Furthermore, the analysis indicates that this improvement is achieved by reducing variance in certain action dimensions, effectively coupling them with other actions as action coordination.

</details>


### [35] [TuringSpace: Grounding Spatial Intelligence in Action with Interactive CAPTCHAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25387)
*Xuwei Ding,Skylar Zhai,Linxin Song,Jiarui Feng,Dongqi Fu,Huajun Xi,Junnan Li,Jingcheng Liang,Jieyu Zhao,Yinglong Xia,Hong Li,Jian Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出交互式验证码环境 TuringSpace（125 个可编程生成的空间任务，需通过点击/拖拽求解），揭示 MLLM 空间智能缺陷：人类 94.1%，GPT-6 Astra 失败 25.9%、Kimi K3 失败 68.0%；失败主因是误读截图，且推理倾向于为错误信念幻觉证据。在该环境上微调 Qwen3.5-9B 后成功率由 1.6% 升至 33.1%，并在七个 OOD 基准上均有提升（VSI-Bench 提升 9.9 点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有验证码基准多已被解决且少有空间属性，而空间基准又大多只做静态判断、不要求执行动作。人类恰是通过"从 2D 视图推断 3D 布局—预测动作后果—观察结果并自我纠正"的闭环来解题，因此需要一个空间化、交互式、且可规模化的测试与训练环境来评估 MLLM 的具身式空间智能。

Method: 构建合成的网页环境 TuringSpace，含 125 个交互式验证码，覆盖五类空间能力，必须以屏幕点击和拖拽求解；每个任务是以程序形式定义的生成器，配备 checker 与 oracle，可无限生成新实例，从而兼作可扩展训练场地。在分析环节对比人类与多个 MLLM 表现并做失败原因归因；随后在该平台上微调 Qwen3.5-9B，并在七个分布外基准上评估迁移效果。

Result: 人类成功率 94.1%；最佳模型 GPT-6 Astra 失败率 25.9%，最佳开源模型 Kimi K3 失败率 68.0%。失败主要源于对截图的误读，且模型推理很少纠正错误信念，反而幻觉出证据来为其辩护。微调后 Qwen3.5-9B 成功率从 1.6% 提升至 33.1%，超过 Kimi K3，并在全部七个 OOD 基准上提升，其中真实室内视频基准 VSI-Bench 提升 9.9 点。

Conclusion: 将空间智能锚定在屏幕动作上，能有效暴露 MLLM 缺失的关键能力，并提供可扩展的学习途径，其收益可迁移至真实场景；作者将公开 TuringSpace 以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Humans act in the visual world by inferring the 3D layout behind a 2D view, predicting how an action changes it, and correcting themselves after seeing the result. Can multimodal large language models (MLLMs) do the same? CAPTCHAs offer a natural test, since humans solve them through this very loop, clicking and dragging on the screen and adjusting after each result. Yet existing CAPTCHA benchmarks are largely solved and rarely spatial, while spatial benchmarks rarely require action. We therefore present TuringSpace, a synthetic web environment of 125 interactive CAPTCHAs that span five spatial abilities and must be solved through clicks and drags on the screen. Every task is a program that generates new instances with a checker and an oracle, so the tasks also form a scalable training gym. Humans solve 94.1% of the tasks, whereas the best model, GPT-6 Astra, still fails 25.9% of them, and the best open-weight model, Kimi K3, fails 68.0%. Our further analysis shows that most failures come from misreading the screenshot, and that model reasoning rarely corrects a wrong belief but hallucinates evidence to defend it. To test whether such spatial abilities can be learned, we fine-tune Qwen3.5-9B on this gym. Its success rate rises from 1.6% to 33.1%, above Kimi K3, and it improves on all seven out-of-distribution benchmarks, including a 9.9-point gain on the real-world indoor videos of VSI-Bench. These results show that grounding spatial intelligence in screen actions exposes what MLLMs lack and offers a scalable way to teach it, with gains that transfer to real-world scenes. We will release TuringSpace to support further study.

</details>


### [36] [Protein Functional Semantic Alignment via Representation Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25396)
*Chuyang Zhou,Bingxin Zhou,Andi Han,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAVR: 以Pfam同源家族共成员关系为离散语义信号的对比式后训练方法，重塑蛋白语言模型表征几何，提升功能预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PLM的似然类预训练目标（掩码语言建模、去噪）只捕捉序列层面规律，表征中功能信号与非功能变异（序列同一性、组成偏差、高频局部motif）纠缠，形成下游功能预测的读出瓶颈。

Method: 提出Functional Semantic Alignment via Representation Rectification (SAVR)：基于Pfam家族共成员关系作为同源引导的离散语义信号，进行对比式后训练；无需任务特定标签，冻结骨干网络评估。

Result: SAVR降低方向性主导，重新分配嵌入维度方差，降低方差集中度，提高嵌入协方差谱的有效维度；在EC基准上取得SOTA，在GO基准上取得有竞争力的结果（冻结骨干评估，后训练未见任务标签）。

Conclusion: 同源引导的对比式后训练能够矫正PLM表征几何，并改进基于序列的蛋白功能预测。

Abstract: Protein language models (PLMs) are commonly pretrained with likelihood-based objectives such as masked language modeling or denoising. These objectives capture sequence-level regularities, but can produce representations in which functional signals are entangled with non-functional variation (e.g., sequence identity, compositional bias, and frequent local motifs), creating a readout bottleneck for downstream function prediction. We introduce Functional Semantic Alignment via Representation Rectification (SAVR), a contrastive post-training method using Pfam family co-membership as a homology-guided discrete semantic signal to reshape PLM representations. SAVR reduces directional dominance and redistributes variance across embedding dimensions, decreasing variance concentration, and increasing effective dimensionality of the embedding covariance spectrum. Without observing any task-specific labels during post-training, SAVR achieves state-of-the-art results on Enzyme Commission (EC) benchmarks and competitive results on Gene Ontology (GO) benchmarks under frozen-backbone evaluation. These results suggest that homology-guided contrastive post-training can rectify PLM representation geometry and improve sequence-based protein function prediction.

</details>


### [37] [Antom 3-in-1 Transformer: A Payment Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25403)
*Weiyu Han,Haoyu Jiang,Jie Sheng,Youxi Pan,Xianglong Liu,Zeqi Ren,Dongquan Yi,Yinhao Liu,Ruobing Zuo,Ze Zhao,Huilin He,Nairong Zhang,Xinyi He,Xiao Tan,Jun Wang,Pan Fang,Wei Zeng,Jianan Su,Kaixin Shen,Jingtong Wu,Hao Li,Shuai Chen,Jiangming Yang,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Antom 3-in-1 Transformer，一个约 7B 参数（每任务路激活约 1.7B）的支付基础模型，统一融合序列、表格与图三种表征用于支付风险检测；通过在蚂蚁国际内部支付数据上预训练获得可复用的行为与关系表征，并在跨业务迁移、少标签场景及亿级规模在线实验中显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 支付风险检测需要同时刻画用户行为序列、结构化（表格）特征与实体间关系（图）信息，而现有方法通常分别建模或依赖业务内标注数据从零训练，导致表征难以复用、跨业务迁移与标签稀缺场景下性能受限。因此作者希望构建一个可预训练的多表征融合基础模型。

Method: 构建 Antom 3-in-1 Transformer，将序列、表格、图三类表征集成于统一架构，总参数约 7B，采用分任务路由激活（每路约 1.7B）。模型主要在蚂蚁国际内部支付数据上进行预训练，以学习可复用的行为表征与关系表征，再迁移至下游支付风险检测任务。

Result: 跨业务迁移实验中，预训练相比同架构从零训练使目标业务指标提升 34.5%；仅使用 25% 训练标签时，预训练模型在不同商户域上的 AP 与 Recall@0.5% 仍超过全标签从零训练基线；在蚂蚁国际亿级规模支付平台为期四个月的在线实验中，相较 XGB-Online 将整体损失率降低 37.88%。

Conclusion: 预训练的多表征统一基础模型能有效复用行为与关系知识，在跨业务迁移、标签稀缺与大规模在线工业场景中均带来显著收益，验证了支付领域基础模型的可行性与实用价值。

Abstract: We report the development of Antom 3-in-1 Transformer, a payment foundation model that integrates sequence, tabular, and graph representations for payment risk detection. The model contains approximately 7B parameters, with approximately 1.7B activated per task route. Antom 3-in-1 Transformer is pretrained to learn reusable behavioral and relational representations, primarily on Ant International's internal payment data. In a cross-business transfer experiment, pretraining improves the target business metric by 34.5% over the same architecture trained from scratch. With only 25% of the training labels, the pretrained model outperforms the full-label scratch baseline in Average Precision (AP) and Recall@0.5% on different merchant domains. In a four-month online experiment on Ant International's billion-scale payment platform, the model reduced the overall loss rate by 37.88% relative to XGB-Online.

</details>


### [38] [FG-GraphBench: Benchmarking and Enhancing Fine-Grained Visual Understanding with Multimodal Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25408)
*Sicheng Liu,Xunkai Li,Ziyu Han,Xingnan Zhang,Ru Zhang,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出细粒度多模态场景图基准 FG-GraphBench 与图表示增强方法 FiGRA，通过将多模态实体与关系特征编码为连续图 token 注入 MLLM，在三个细粒度任务上显著优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度视觉理解要求 MLLM 识别小视觉实体及其关系。现有场景图基准对细粒度实体及其关系的覆盖不足，且现有方法尚未在 MLLM 中有效利用图结构信息。

Method: 构建 FG-GraphBench，含 FG-QA、FG-Caption、FG-Description 三个互补任务，采用两阶段流水线发现并定位细粒度实体、建立关系以构造多模态场景图（MSGs）。基于此提出 FiGRA，采用两阶段训练：将多模态实体与关系特征编码为连续图 token 并集成进 MLLM。

Result: FiGRA 在 FG-GraphBench 三个任务上显著超越强基线；进一步分析验证了基准的有效性，并表明保留细粒度实体与关系信息对视觉理解至关重要。

Conclusion: 细粒度场景图结构化信息能有效提升 MLLM 的细粒度视觉理解能力，配套基准与图表示增强方法相互支撑。

Abstract: Fine-grained visual understanding requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to recognize small visual entities and understand their relationships. Scene graphs offer a natural way to represent this information, organizing visual entities as nodes and their relationships as edges. However, we identify two challenges: existing scene graph benchmarks provide insufficient coverage of fine-grained entities and the relationships among them, and existing methods for fine-grained visual understanding have yet to effectively leverage such graph-structured information in MLLMs. To address these challenges, we introduce Fine-Grained Multimodal Scene Graph Benchmark (FG-GraphBench) and FiGRA. FG-GraphBench is a benchmark comprising three complementary tasks: fine-grained visual question answering (FG-QA), image captioning (FG-Caption), and image description generation (FG-Description). We develop a two-stage construction pipeline that discovers and grounds fine-grained entities and establishes their relations to construct the fine-grained multimodal scene graphs (MSGs) in FG-GraphBench. Building on these MSGs, we propose FiGRA, a Fine-grained Graph Representation Augmentation method for MLLMs. FiGRA adopts a two-stage training strategy. It encodes multimodal entity and relation features as continuous graph tokens, and integrates them into the MLLM. Experiments show that FiGRA significantly outperforms strong baselines across three tasks of FG-GraphBench. Further analyses confirm the effectiveness of FG-GraphBench and highlight the importance of preserving fine-grained entity and relation information for visual understanding.

</details>


### [39] [From Sequence Uncertainty to Phenotype Hypotheses: Uncertainty-Conditioned Inference over Biomedical Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25422)
*Huixin Zhan,Jason H. Moore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出不确定性条件的路径能量框架，将基因组基础模型的序列预测与校准后的预测不确定性转化为图支持的表型假设显式分布，无需患者级表型监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因组基础模型提供变异级证据，但把该证据转化为表型假设需要对结构化推理全过程的不确定性进行建模；现有方法缺乏将证据质量显式纳入假设生成的机制。

Method: 构建可解释学习能量对基因—疾病—表型路径打分；查询不确定性控制目标温度（temperature）；路径概率聚合为表型假设，并在可枚举候选集上做精确归一化作为参考，用于评估Hastings校正的学习修订算子。

Result: AlzKB与Hetionet上表型级熵与上游不确定性Spearman相关为0.951和0.878；消融证实不确定性条件化贡献；掩蔽边恢复优于随机及度匹配概率置换对照；不确定性依赖温度训练在留出recall@10上优于同量级常数温度；基因留出下24/30个图掩码优于DistMult嵌入（0.183 vs 0.123），差异主要源于三个多变异基因。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了从基因组预测到图支持表型假设的不确定性感知接口，兼顾可解释概率分配与界定明确的留出关联恢复能力。

Abstract: Genomic foundation models provide variant-level evidence, but connecting that evidence to phenotype hypotheses requires accounting for uncertainty throughout structured reasoning. We introduce an uncertainty-conditioned path-energy framework that converts sequence predictions and calibrated predictive uncertainty into explicit distributions over graph-supported phenotype hypotheses, without patient-level phenotype supervision. An interpretable learned energy scores admissible gene--disease--phenotype routes, query uncertainty controls the target temperature, and path probabilities are aggregated into phenotype hypotheses. This formulation makes evidence quality an explicit determinant of hypothesis breadth while retaining traceable multi-path support. Exact normalization over enumerable candidate sets provides a reference for evaluating a Hastings-corrected learned revision operator. On AlzKB and Hetionet, phenotype-level entropy tracks upstream uncertainty, with Spearman correlations of 0.951 and 0.878, respectively; ablations identify the contribution of uncertainty conditioning. In masked-edge recovery, the model exceeds chance and degree-matched probability-permutation controls. Training the energy under the uncertainty-dependent temperature yields higher held-out recall@10 than training under a constant temperature of comparable magnitude. Under gene hold-out, recall@10 exceeds that of a trained DistMult embedding on 24 of 30 graph masks (0.183 versus 0.123); this difference is attributable mainly to three genes with many variants and is not established for all genes. These results establish an uncertainty-aware interface from genomic predictions to graph-supported phenotype hypotheses that combines interpretable probability allocation with held-out association recovery of explicitly stated scope.

</details>


### [40] [Coordinate Basis Linear Bandits with Sublinear Regret over Low-Rank Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25424)
*Hantao Yang,Hong Xie,Defu Lian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出聚类机制与坐标基线性 bandit（CBL）算法，在高维有限臂线性 bandit 中通过维护簇列空间的精确正交基与低维坐标表示，将每轮更新复杂度降至 $O(\bar r^2)$，并在无谱尾风险下保证次线性遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性 bandit 中特征维数 $d$ 增大导致每轮更新计算代价 $O(d^2)$。SOFUL 等固定规模矩阵草图低秩近似虽降低计算量，但当数据谱尾较重且近似秩选取不当时，可能产生空洞的线性遗憾，缺乏遗憾收敛保证。

Method: 引入聚类机制并提出坐标基线性 bandit（CBL）：在每个簇内维护该簇列空间的精确正交基及低维坐标表示，从而完整保留协方差信息；为每个簇设置哨兵（sentinel）以实现簇切换。

Result: 每轮更新计算量由 $O(d^2)$ 降至 $O(\bar r^2)$，其中 $\bar r=\min(r,l)$，$r$ 为数据内在秩，$l<d$ 为可调秩上界；同时获得稳健的次线性遗憾，且不受谱尾问题影响。

Conclusion: CBL 在保持遗憾收敛保证的同时显著降低高维线性 bandit 的计算开销，避免了固定秩草图方法的谱尾脆弱性，实现了效率与理论保证的兼顾。

Abstract: We study the problem of computational efficiency for linear bandits in high-dimensional settings with a finite arm set. In linear bandits, the increase in the dimension $d$ of the feature vectors leads to growing computational costs of $O(d^2)$ at each round of update. Traditional low-rank approximation methods such as SOFUL reduce computation via fixed-size matrix sketching, yet run the risk of incurring vacuous linear regret when the spectral tail of the data is heavy and the approximation rank is inadequately selected. To guarantee regret convergence and effectively reduce computational costs, we introduce a clustering mechanism and propose the Coordinate Basis Linear Bandit (CBL) algorithm. Our method preserves the full covariance information in each cluster by maintaining an exact orthonormal basis of the cluster's column space together with a low-dimensional coordinate representation, which guarantees robust sublinear regret without spectral-tail vulnerabilities. It performs cluster switching by assigning a sentinel for each cluster, and reduces per-round update computation to $O(\bar r^2)$ in the cluster's effective dimension $\bar r=\min(r,l)$, where $r$ is the intrinsic rank of the data and $l<d$ is a tunable rank bound.

</details>


### [41] [Interference Bounds and Merge-Aware Training Dynamics for Composable Fine-Tuned Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25430)
*Alireza Moayedikia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将任务向量合并（模型组合）的可组合性形式化为一个由曲率交叉重叠与更新符号对齐构成的干涉泛函；证明合并损失超额受该泛函控制，二次情形下泛函等于超额损失，并给出曲率引导正则化的收敛与上界控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数更新组合（任务向量合并）已成为多任务训练的标准替代方案，但其何时可组合、合并后损失退化如何度量缺乏理论刻画；本文旨在建立可组合性的定量判据，并在训练阶段主动控制这一退化。

Method: 定义任务向量集合的可组合性，构造基于跨任务曲率重叠与更新符号对齐的干涉泛函，证明合并模型超额损失受其控制；在二次情形下泛函精确等于超额损失，判据充要，且测度的是曲率度量而非欧氏度量下的任务向量夹角；设计由内存高效优化器所维护的曲率统计构造的曲率引导正则项，在非凸 O(1/√T) 速率下逐迭代约束位移；通过基础统计与任务曲率间的单一 Loewner 比较，将控制转化为干涉泛函上界（与惩罚系数成反比）。

Result: 精确非二次模型实验验证了位移预算与泛函上界，显示强惩罚下一阶项占主导（因正则化解非任务损失驻点，该项不被同样削减），曲率引导惩罚略优于各向同性惩罚；三随机种子 Transformer 实验中，该泛函与合并退化的秩相关性与欧氏位移范数相当，未更强。

Conclusion: 干涉泛函为任务向量合并提供了充要的局部判据与可训练的控制手段，但强正则下一阶项与欧氏范数在实际 Transformer 上的同等预测力表明其经验优势有限，需进一步处理非驻点项与曲率估计质量。

Abstract: Combining fine-tuned models by composing their parameter updates has become a standard alternative to multi-task training. We formalize composability as a property of a collection of task vectors and prove that the excess loss of the merged model is controlled by an interference functional of the cross-task curvature overlap and the signed alignment of the updates. In the quadratic regime this functional equals the excess loss, giving a criterion that is necessary as well as sufficient and that measures the angle between task vectors in the curvature metric rather than the Euclidean one. Fine-tuning under a curvature-guided regularizer -- constructed from curvature statistics of the kind memory-efficient optimizers maintain -- keeps displacement in the base curvature metric below a ceiling set by the penalty coefficient at every iterate, at the standard $O(1/\sqrt{T})$ non-convex rate. Under a single Loewner comparison between the base statistic and the task curvatures, this control converts into a ceiling on the interference functional, inversely proportional to the penalty coefficient, so that the curvature term of the bound is controlled during training. A first-order term, present because regularized solutions are not stationary for the task loss, is not reduced in the same way. Experiments on an exact non-quadratic model confirm the budget and the ceiling, show the first-order term dominating for strong penalties, and find the curvature-guided penalty slightly better than an isotropic one. In a three-seed transformer study the functional is rank-correlated with merging degradation, but no more strongly than the Euclidean displacement norm.

</details>


### [42] [Inverse ECG Reconstruction via a Preconditioned Variational Quantum Linear Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25450)
*Xizhuo Zhang,Chao Lu,Bing Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个混合量子-经典逆心电重建框架：先用拉普拉斯正则化预编码降维并改善条件数，再用一致变分量子线性求解器（VQLS）求解，并理论证明全过程保持原问题精确解，在真实心-躯干几何上验证了重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆心电（inverse ECG）需从体表电位重建心外膜电位，需求解稠密且严重病态的线性系统，计算成本随心脏网格细化快速增长；作者希望借助量子计算求解此类线性系统的新能力。

Method: 1）提出拉普拉斯正则化预编码（Laplacian-regularized pre-encoding）步骤，在降维的同时改善系统条件数；2）将降维后的系统编码并用相干变分量子线性求解器（coherent VQLS）求解；3）给出严格理论证明，说明框架在各计算阶段均保持原重建问题的精确解。

Result: 在真实心脏-躯干几何上的实验表明，该方法具备准确进行逆心电重建的显著能力。

Conclusion: 结果凸显量子求解器在无创心脏成像中的潜力，为量子增强心脏评估及其他医疗应用的研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Inverse electrocardiography (ECG) reconstructs heart-surface potentials from body-surface measurements, enabling noninvasive mapping of cardiac electrical activity. However, the reconstruction requires solving dense and ill-conditioned linear systems, whose computational cost grows rapidly with the refined heart mesh. Quantum computing offers new opportunities for solving such systems. This paper presents a hybrid quantum-classical framework for inverse ECG reconstruction, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first to apply a quantum linear solver to this problem. We first propose a Laplacian-regularized pre-encoding procedure to reduce the dimension of the system while improving its conditioning. Second, the resulting reduced system is encoded and solved using a coherent variational quantum linear solver (VQLS). Finally, we provide a rigorous theoretical proof establishing that the proposed framework preserves the exact solution of the original reconstruction problem throughout all stages of the computational process. Experimental results on a realistic heart-torso geometry demonstrate that our approach holds significant ability for accurate inverse ECG reconstruction. These findings highlight the potential of quantum solvers for noninvasive cardiac imaging, providing a foundation for further research into quantum-enhanced cardiac assessment and other healthcare applications.

</details>


### [43] [FamilySpace: From Emergent Chemical Semantics to Controllable Crystal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25452)
*Haowei Hua,Irwin King,Wanyu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发现晶体VAE的原子级潜空间会自发按元素族聚集，称为"涌现化学语义"，并据此提出FamilySpace，通过族索引的多源分布与族匹配的流模型实现生成晶体的元素族组成可控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往潜空间晶体生成模型（VAE+流模型）主要关注生成晶体质量，而未深入探究原子级潜空间的结构及其生成效用。

Method: 先揭示晶体VAE隐式按元素族组织潜表示；再为每个元素族分配独立的源分布分量，构成族索引的多源分布，并用按族匹配的源-目标配对训练流模型；推理时由用户指定族模板为每个原子级表示选择源分量。

Result: 在MP-20与MPTS-52上，FamilySpace取得有竞争力的生成性能，同时满足预设的元素族组成；选用特定族模板可提高生成晶体被预测为稳定或亚稳的比例。

Conclusion: 潜空间组成控制可在无需强化学习等额外优化手段的情况下，支持对潜在化学区域的有针对性探索。

Abstract: Latent crystal generative models typically combine a variational autoencoder (VAE) for learning atom-wise representations with a flow model that transports a simple source distribution to the learned latent distribution. However, previous studies have mainly focused on the quality of generated crystals, leaving the structure and generative utility of the atom-wise latent space largely unexplored. In this work, we show that even a vanilla crystal VAE implicitly organizes atom-wise latent representations according to elemental families, with different families occupying largely separated regions of the latent space. We term this phenomenon emergent chemical semantics. This structure suggests that sampling from a family-specific latent region can bias the decoder toward atoms from the corresponding elemental family. Building on this insight, we propose FamilySpace, a framework for controlling the elemental-family composition of generated crystals. FamilySpace assigns each elemental family a distinct source component, forming a family-indexed multi-source distribution, and trains a flow model using source-target pairs matched by family. In inference, a user-specified family template selects the source component for each atom-wise representation, enabling direct control over the family composition of generated crystals. Experiments on MP-20 and MPTS-52 show that FamilySpace achieves competitive crystal-generation performance while matching prescribed elemental-family compositions. Moreover, selected family templates increase the fraction of generated crystals predicted to be stable or metastable, demonstrating that latent-space composition control can support targeted exploration of promising chemical regions without additional optimization techniques such as reinforcement learning. Code is available at https://github.com/hhw11/FamilySpace

</details>


### [44] [Dimension-Free Buffer Bounds for Compressed-Momentum Error Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25463)
*Alireza Moayedikia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过将压缩动量重注入压缩前累加器，可使误差反馈界完全不含压缩器的收缩常数 r/p，误差缓冲区范数满足与维度 m、n 及秩 r 无关的递推界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有误差反馈分析用压缩器收缩常数（最优秩-r近似的 r/p）界定记忆缓冲区，该常数渗入收敛率，导致率依赖于维度和秩。作者希望消除该常数，刻画一个与维度、秩无关的误差反馈界。

Method: 让压缩后的动量β₁M_{t-1}重新注入压缩前累加器，使其成为投影中的可行竞争点；仅利用压缩器是到闭锥上的度量投影这一性质，得到‖E_t‖_F ≤ (1-β₁)‖G_t‖_F + γ‖E_{t-1}‖_F。基于轨迹追踪形式，无需对梯度作假设，覆盖top-k选择与截断SVD，并对投影集内任意选择成立（不要求奇异值穿越条件）。另将完整速率转移的泄漏项用步长加权梯度质量∑η_u‖G_u‖_F界定。

Result: 在标准分析的两阶段模板中，将Stage-2常数由Θ(p²/r²)降为Θ(1)，对比基于收缩的最锐界Θ(p/r)；泄漏项界在√2因子内是紧的，代价是加性O(log²T)项而非更慢的速率。BERT微调（秩比32/768）中反馈衰减带来的验证F₁损失小于0.008。

Conclusion: 压缩器收缩常数在误差反馈界中并非本质必需：通过压缩动量重注入，可获得维度与秩无关的误差递推，显著改进Stage-2常数，且仅以加性对数项为代价，实践中精度损失可忽略。

Abstract: Analyses of error feedback bound the memory buffer through a compressor contraction constant -- $r/p$ for the best rank-$r$ approximation of a $p$-dimensional spectrum -- which survives into the rate. We show it need not appear at all. When the compressed momentum is re-injected into the pre-compression accumulator, the previous compressed momentum $β_1 M_{t-1}$ is an admissible competitor in the projection, and the buffer obeys $\|E_t\|_F \le (1-β_1)\|G_t\|_F + γ\|E_{t-1}\|_F$, free of the dimensions $m,n$ and of the rank $r$. The argument uses only that the compressor is a metric projection onto a closed cone, so it covers top-$k$ selection as well as truncated SVD, holds for every selection from the projection set with no condition on singular-value crossings, and in its trajectory-tracking form assumes nothing about the gradients. In the two-stage template of the standard analyses it replaces a Stage-2 constant of order $p^2/r^2$ by one of order $1$, against $Θ(p/r)$ for the sharpest contraction-based bounds. Transferring the full rate costs a leakage term, which we bound by the step-weighted gradient mass $\sum_{u\le t}η_u\|G_u\|_F$ and show tight up to a factor $\sqrt{2}$; the price is an additive $O(\log^2 T)$ floor, not a slower rate. On BERT fine-tuning at rank ratio $32/768$ the feedback decay costs under $0.008$ validation $F_1$.

</details>


### [45] [WeightCode: Installing New Capabilities into Neural Networks via Weight-Space Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25476)
*Bo Li,Taoyong Cui,Bob Zhang,Mengran Li,Chengyang Zhang,Yang Zhang,Pheng Ann Heng,Wanli Ouyang,Zhenfei Yin,Yingcheng Wu,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Weight-Space Programming (WSP)范式及WeightCode框架，用冻结语言模型编写可执行的"权重程序"，直接读写/构造/修改已训练网络权重，无需梯度训练即可安装新能力，且程序可复用、可叠加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络将能力编码于权重中，但用显式任务知识扩展这些能力仍是根本难题；现有方式依赖梯度微调，成本高且难以复用与累积能力。作者希望探索一条不通过梯度训练、而是通过程序化操作权重来为已训练网络安装新能力的路径。

Method: WeightCode框架：由冻结的语言模型根据任务知识与目标网络现有权重，生成可执行的权重程序，直接读取、构造或修改网络权重以安装新计算；安装后新能力独立于语言模型运行。评估涵盖命令组合与pickup动作、ImageNet鲁棒性（对比8种微调方法，在匹配样本、检查与保持性约束下比较），并用因果干预分析ViT中的噪声校正门控，测试跨5个独立训练ViT的重跑复用性，以及在已编辑网络上追加程序。

Result: 未见过的命令组合精确匹配从0%升至99.8%，新pickup动作成功率从0%升至98.4%，原有命令与导航能力保持；ImageNet上四个权重程序带来8.84–17.62个百分点的净增益且保持干净精度，优于8种微调方法中的最佳者；因果干预显示安装的噪声校正门控驱动鲁棒性提升，其选择性激活保持干净精度；在5个独立ViT上重跑（无额外语言模型调用），十个程序平均保留源网络增益的83–110%，而复制其固定权重编辑增益可忽略；为已编辑网络编写的六个程序再增5.23–14.50点对比鲁棒性并保留先前噪声增益。

Conclusion: WSP被确立为一条通过可复用权重程序在已训练神经网络中获取与累积能力的新途径：新能力可无梯度安装、脱离语言模型独立运行、跨网络复用并可叠加累积，同时保持原有能力与干净精度。

Abstract: Neural networks encode learned capabilities in their weights, yet extending those capabilities with explicit task knowledge remains a fundamental challenge. Here we show that Weight-Space Programming (WSP), in which executable programs read, construct or modify a network's weights directly, can install new capabilities within trained neural networks. WeightCode, a WSP framework, uses a frozen language model to write executable weight programs that install new computations into trained neural networks using task knowledge and their existing weights, without gradient-based training. Once installed, the new capabilities run independently of the language model. Given a grammar or eight demonstrations, weight programs enable previously unseen command compositions (0% to 99.8% exact match) and a new pickup action (0% to 98.4% success), while preserving the original command and navigation capabilities. On ImageNet, four weight programs achieve net gains of 8.84-17.62 points while preserving clean accuracy, outperforming the best of eight fine-tuning methods under matched examples, checks, and retention constraints. In a Vision Transformer, causal interventions show that installed noise-correction gates drive robustness gains, while their selective activation preserves clean accuracy. Rerun on five independently trained Vision Transformers without further language model calls, ten weight programs retain 83-110% of their source-network gains on average, whereas copying their fixed weight edits yields negligible gains. Six programs written for an already edited network add 5.23-14.50 points of contrast robustness while retaining the previously installed noise gain. Together, these results establish WSP as a new route to acquiring and accumulating capabilities in trained neural networks through reusable weight programs.

</details>


### [46] [Sequential Probability Assignment against Smoothed Adversaries with Unknown Base Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25514)
*Ziyi Liu,Daniel M. Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基测度 μ 未知、且无 Lipschitz 假设下的平滑适定数据序列概率分配问题，给出了针对自适应与无意识平滑对手的遗憾上界（分别由经验 ℓ∞ 与 ℓ2 Hellinger 熵控制），并证明其在若干自然类上本质紧，从而揭示 μ 已知与 μ 未知两种情形之间的困难性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全对抗设定下的硬度结果过于悲观，平滑在线学习通过约束对手从相对固定基测度 μ 有界密度的分布中生成上下文来规避这一悲观性。既有工作多强假设 μ 为学习器已知（Block et al. 2024、Blanchard 2025 为例外但带有 Lipschitz 条件），本文旨在去除"μ 已知"与"Lipschitz"两项限制。

Method: 在 μ 未知、数据平滑且适定的设定下研究序列概率分配（即对数损失在线学习）；对自适应与无意识两类平滑对手分别构造基于截断经验 Hellinger 覆盖的算法，并配合一般性下界论证。

Result: 自适应对手的遗憾上界由经验 ℓ∞ Hellinger 熵控制；无意识对手的上界改进为经验 ℓ2 Hellinger 熵（即刻画 i.i.d. 学习复杂度的量，Bilodeau et al. 2023）。一般性下界表明这些上界对某些自然函数类本质紧。

Conclusion: 在 μ 未知且无 Lipschitz 条件下仍可获得近乎最优的遗憾保证，且下界揭示了平滑在线学习在 μ 已知与 μ 未知两种情形之间的困难性分离。

Abstract: Smoothed online learning has recently been studied as a way to bypass hardness results for the fully adversarial setting, which can be overly pessimistic. In this framework, the adversary is constrained to generate contexts from distributions having a bounded density with respect to some fixed base measure $μ$. Prior work makes the strong assumption that $μ$ is known to the learner, with notable exceptions including Block et al. (2024) and Blanchard (2025). In this paper, we study sequential probability assignment (a.k.a., online learning with log loss) with smooth, well-specified data in the more general setting where $μ$ is \emph{unknown}, without the Lipschitz condition imposed in the work of Block et al. (2024) and Blanchard (2025). We prove regret upper bounds against both adaptive and oblivious smoothed adversaries: the adaptive bound is controlled by empirical $\ell_\infty$ Hellinger entropy, while the oblivious bound improves this dependence to empirical $\ell_2$ Hellinger entropy, the notion that has been shown to characterize the complexity of learning with i.i.d. data (Bilodeau et al., 2023). Both bounds are obtained through algorithms based on truncated empirical Hellinger covers. We complement these results with a general lower bound, showing that our upper bounds are essentially tight for some natural classes, which implies a separation in the difficulty of smoothed online learning between regimes where $μ$ is known and where it is unknown.

</details>


### [47] [Towards Autoregressive Neural Network Parameter Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25538)
*Bedionita Soro,Bruno Andreis,Song Chong,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出指令引导参数生成（IGPG），将网络参数生成建模为自回归问题：先用VQ-VAE量化预训练权重，再训练前缀条件自回归生成器，实现跨数据集与跨架构的参数生成与迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有权重生成方法难以同时学习多样模型的参数分布、在单一生成器中编码异构架构、并具备分布外泛化能力。作者希望借助LLM在文本生成中表现出的零样本泛化能力，将预训练参数编码到离散域以实现可迁移的权重生成。

Method: 两阶段框架：1）用VQ-VAE对预训练权重进行量化，得到离散权重token；2）在token序列上训练前缀条件（prefix-conditioned）的自回归生成器。推理时，生成的权重token经VQ-VAE解码器映射回参数空间。

Result: 在分布内生成、跨数据集迁移与跨架构设置下评估：分布内生成取得有竞争力的性能；对留出数据集与架构配置可提供有效的参数初始化，经适配后下游性能优于随机初始化。

Conclusion: 离散自回归建模为学习并在预训练神经网络参数集合间迁移结构提供了有前景的框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently established a new paradigm in machine learning, demonstrating exceptional zero-shot generalization and complex reasoning capabilities. Concurrently, neural network parameter generation has emerged as a promising avenue for rapid task adaptation and model synthesis. Existing weight generation techniques, however, struggle to jointly learn the distribution of diverse models, to encode heterogeneous architectures within a single generator, and to generalize out of distribution. We therefore ask whether pretrained parameters can be encoded into a discrete domain so that the zero-shot generalization LLMs exhibit in text generation can be transferred to weight generation. Following this view, we propose an LLM-inspired approach, Instruction-Guided Parameter Generation (IGPG), that formulates network parameter generation as an autoregressive modeling problem. We first quantize pretrained weights with a VQ-VAE, then train a prefix-conditioned autoregressive generator over the resulting tokens. At inference, the generated weight tokens are mapped back to parameter space by the VQ-VAE decoder. We evaluate the framework across in-distribution generation, cross-dataset transfer, and cross-architecture settings. The proposed method achieves competitive performance in in-distribution generation and provides useful parameter initializations for held-out datasets and architectural configurations, improving downstream performance over random initialization after adaptation. These results suggest that discrete autoregressive modeling provides a promising framework for learning and transferring structure across collections of pretrained neural network parameters.

</details>


### [48] [Is a Description Worth a Thousand Demonstrations? Specificity and Reliability of Task Descriptions in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25560)
*Bruce Nguyen,Bei Jiang,Linglong Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将ICL中的任务描述建模为具有特定性与可靠性两个属性的信息先验，并用贝叶斯有效样本量(ESS)量化描述等价于多少示例，理论与实验表明元训练的Transformer接近贝叶斯最优，而预训练语言模型在无推理时利用高特定描述不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICL提示既可含示例也可含任务描述，但描述性文本在理论上如何起作用仍缺乏理解；先前贝叶斯分析主要针对示例，未覆盖描述。

Method: 在ICL的贝叶斯视角下，将任务描述刻画为具有"特定性"和"可靠性"的先验；引入贝叶斯统计中的有效样本量(ESS)，把描述的预测价值折算为等价的示例数量；在in-context线性回归任务上作理论推导，并对比元训练Transformer与预训练语言模型（含/不含推理）与贝叶斯最优预测器的ESS匹配程度。

Result: 理论结果：可靠描述的ESS随其特定性及任务维度增长；可能无关的描述若缺少示例，其特定性几乎不带来收益。实验：元训练Transformer在训练分布上接近贝叶斯最优ESS；预训练语言模型在无推理作答时对高特定描述利用不足，多数仅在启用推理时接近贝叶斯最优预测器。

Conclusion: 把ICL的贝叶斯分析从示例推广到描述，ESS可作为衡量描述预测价值的统一指标，并揭示现有模型在利用描述先验上的差异与推理的作用。

Abstract: A prompt for in-context learning (ICL) can describe a task in addition to, or instead of, demonstrating it through examples. Yet how task descriptions work remains poorly understood theoretically. Through the Bayesian view of ICL, we model descriptions as informative priors characterized by their specificity and reliability. We adapt the effective sample size (ESS) from Bayesian statistics, quantifying a description's predictive value as an equivalent number of demonstrations. For in-context linear regression, we show that a reliable description's ESS grows with its specificity and with the task's dimension. A description that may be irrelevant, however, gains little from its specificity unless demonstrations are given. In practice, do in-context learners use descriptions as a Bayes-optimal predictor would? Our experiments show that meta-trained Transformers come close to the Bayes-optimal ESS when tested on their training distribution. Pretrained language models, however, make too little use of highly specific descriptions when answering without reasoning; most of them closely match the Bayes-optimal predictor only when reasoning is enabled. These results extend the Bayesian analysis of ICL from demonstrations to descriptions.

</details>


### [49] [Reinforcement-Aligned Probabilistic Weather Forecasting with Discrete Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25570)
*Jinhan Li,Zhisheng Chen,Yuxuan Li,Yuan Gao,Fan Xu,Hao Wu,Jinlong Du,Kun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WeatherCraft 通过离散天气 token 统一概率生成与强化学习对齐，将 GPT 式预训练—后训练范式引入全球中期天气预报，在 ERA5 上显著优于确定性基线并大幅加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球中期天气预报需要高效生成集合以刻画未来不确定性，同时要服务极端事件预警、跨圈层耦合模拟等下游任务。现有方法难以同时兼顾高效集合生成与任务可对齐性。

Method: 1）多尺度 tokenizer 将大气状态表示为粗到细的 token 层级；2）Transformer 建模跨时间与跨尺度的因果依赖，并在每个尺度内并行预测 token；3）直接采样生成多样化集合；4）利用精确 token 对数概率实现 GRPO，最大化下游任务奖励，无需对评估器或耦合模型求导；5）KV 缓存加速推理。

Result: ERA5 基准上，八成员集合均值使第 15 天归一化 RMSE 较最强确定性基线降低 17.6%；配合 KV 缓存较 GenCast 推理加速约 18 倍；直接 fair-CRPS 适配提升集合概率技巧；基于奖励的后训练改善持续性陆面温度极端与耦合海洋热极端的预报。

Conclusion: 该工作确立离散自回归建模可作为可扩展、可任务对齐的地球系统基础模型的通用基座。

Abstract: Global medium-range weather forecasting must not only generate ensembles efficiently to characterize uncertainty in future states, but also serve downstream applications such as extreme-event warnings and cross-sphere coupled simulations. We introduce WeatherCraft, a framework that unifies probabilistic generation and reinforcement learning alignment through discrete weather tokens, bringing the GPT-style pretraining--post-training paradigm to global medium-range weather forecasting. A multiscale tokenizer represents atmospheric states as a coarse-to-fine token hierarchy, while a Transformer models causal dependencies across time and scale and predicts tokens within each scale in parallel. Direct sampling generates diverse ensembles, and exact token log-probabilities enable Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to maximize downstream task rewards without differentiating through evaluators or coupled models. On the ERA5 benchmark, WeatherCraft's eight-member ensemble mean reduces day-15 normalized RMSE by 17.6% relative to the strongest evaluated deterministic baseline and, with KV caching, achieves an approximately $18\times$ inference speedup over GenCast. Direct fair-CRPS adaptation improves ensemble probabilistic skill, while reward-based post-training improves forecasts of persistent land temperature extremes and coupled ocean heat extremes. This work establishes discrete autoregressive modeling as a general foundation for scalable, task-alignable Earth system foundation models.

</details>


### [50] [Design and Evaluation of Epira: An Automated Interview System for Case Investigation and Contact Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25577)
*Ishita Asnani,Shaghayegh Fazliani,Zachary Ryan,Kriyoshig Prasai,Chirag Kumar,Indra Gonzalez Ojeda,Marc Lipsitch,Madeleine Udell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Epira是一个由规则控制器约束的LLM访谈系统，用于多疾病病例调查与密接追踪，在模拟与真人研究中表现出高字段完整度、可用性与信任度，且本地部署与云端表现相近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统病例调查和密接追踪依赖人工，成本高；网页表单在受访者需先澄清问题时难以推进，开放式聊天机器人又可能偏离既定流程。因此需要一种既能保持协议合规、又能自然对话的系统。

Method: Epira将LLM对话与基于规则的控制器结合，强制必需问题、安全转接与结构化病例记录，并可通过上传问卷适配新疾病。测试本地Qwen2.5与云端GPT-5.4后端。研究1：108个模拟病例（麻疹、淋病），含真实字段与密接信息，对比Epira与开放式条件，共864次会话。研究2：72名志愿者参与2×2（疾病×模型）访谈，评估完成率、可用性、可接受性与信任度。

Result: 研究1中，本地LLM版Epira捕获88.9%的必需字段和60.1%的真实密接；开放式访谈比同模型Epira少恢复31.1–41.9个百分点的必需字段。研究2完成率100%，改编SUS均值82.95±16.17（0–100），信任度均值3.85±0.77（1–5）。

Conclusion: 协议控制对完整性的影响大于模型选择；本地与云端部署记录相似，支持隐私保护下的本地使用，并对转接访谈和遗漏密接进行人工复核；系统在多样披露条件下可恢复必需字段与密接，并获得良好的可用性与信任评价。

Abstract: Objective: Case investigation and contact tracing are labor-intensive. Web forms struggle when respondents need clarification before disclosing contacts, and open-ended chatbots may deviate from protocols. We developed and evaluated Epira, a protocol-controlled large language model (LLM) interviewer for multi-disease case investigation and contact tracing.
  Materials and Methods: Epira pairs LLM dialogue with a rule-based controller enforcing required questions, safety handoffs, and a structured case record, and adapts to new diseases by questionnaire upload. Local (Qwen2.5) and cloud (GPT-5.4) backends were tested. Study 1 used 108 simulated cases with ground-truth fields and contacts for measles and gonorrhea under Epira and open-ended conditions (864 sessions). Study 2 enrolled 72 volunteers in a 2$\times$2 disease-by-model interview assessing completion, usability, acceptability, and trust.
  Results: Across Study 1, Epira with a local LLM captured 88.9% of required fields and 60.1% of ground-truth contacts. Open-ended interviewers recovered 31.1-41.9 percentage points fewer required fields than the same models in Epira. In Study 2, completion was 100%; mean adapted System Usability Scale (SUS) was 82.95 $\pm$ 16.17 on a 0-100 scale and mean trust was 3.85 $\pm$ 0.77 on a 1-5 scale.
  Discussion: Protocol control drove completeness more than model choice. Local and cloud deployments produced similar records, supporting privacy-preserving local use with human review of routed interviews and missed contacts.
  Conclusion: Epira recovers required fields and contacts under diverse disclosure conditions, works with local and cloud LLMs, and received favorable usability and trust ratings.

</details>


### [51] [From Data Alignment to Parameter Generation: Few-Shot Cross-City Traffic Prediction with Conditional Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25592)
*Shengchun Wang,Yuhang Lin,Tong Li,Pengyuan Zhou,Andy Chi Lok Tai,Wenjie Chen,Zhu Xiao,Zhu Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出条件扩散参数生成框架（CDPG），不做原始数据对齐，而是学习源城市高性能预测器参数的条件分布，并为目标城市基站直接生成节点级模型参数，在三个大规模数据集上较SOTA跨城少样本基线最高提升9.72%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动流量预测对容量规划、拥塞缓解和节能运行至关重要，但许多城市/区域数据稀缺（新部署或测量不足），使预测成为少样本学习问题。现有跨城方案主要依赖数据空间对齐匹配流量分布，在真实分布漂移、测量噪声和城市特定偏差下较为脆弱。

Method: CDPG 学习源城市高性能预测器参数的条件分布，直接为目标城市生成节点特定参数。为可靠适配，参数生成以多模态时空上下文为条件：空间环境线索由卫星影像与LLM语义描述结合获得；复杂时序行为通过无监督模式聚类概括。这些上下文指导为每个基站的运行环境定制参数。

Result: 在三个大规模数据集上，CDPG 在标准预测指标上稳定优于SOTA跨城少样本基线，最高提升9.72%，并在少样本多步预测中保持鲁棒。代码开源于 https://github.com/LingoodBoy/CDPG。

Conclusion: 以参数空间的条件生成替代数据空间对齐，是缓解跨城分布漂移的有效范式；多模态上下文条件化可提升少样本移动流量预测的鲁棒性与精度。

Abstract: Accurate mobile traffic forecasting is crucial for network service management, supporting proactive capacity provisioning, congestion mitigation, and energy-efficient operation. However, many cities and regions are data-scarce (e.g., newly deployed or poorly instrumented areas), thus making traffic prediction a few-shot learning problem and limiting the practicality of standard data-driven models. Existing cross-city solutions primarily rely on data-space alignment to match traffic distributions between data-rich and data-scarce cities, but such alignment is often brittle under real-world distribution shifts, measurement noise, and city-specific bias. To address these limitations, we propose a Conditional Diffusion-based Parameter Generation (CDPG) framework. Instead of aligning raw data, CDPG learns the conditional distribution of high-performing predictor parameters for source cities and directly generates node-specific model parameters for the target city. To support reliable adaptation for network traffic prediction, CDPG conditions parameter generation on multi-modal spatio-temporal context. Specifically, CDPG captures spatial environment cues by combining satellite imagery with large language model (LLM)-derived semantic descriptions, and summarizes complex temporal behaviors via unsupervised pattern clustering. These contextual components guide CDPG in producing parameters tailored to each base station's operational context, thereby improving robustness under cross-city and cross-region shifts. Extensive experiments on three large-scale datasets show that CDPG consistently outperforms state-of-the-art cross-city few-shot baselines by up to 9.72% on standard forecasting metrics and remains robust for few-shot multi-step prediction. The code is available at https://github.com/LingoodBoy/CDPG.

</details>


### [52] [Bradley-Terry Training Can Distort Outcome Probabilities of Initially Accurate Language World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25594)
*Xinze Shi,Litian Zhang,Zhenjie Su,Zelong Yu,Yuting Zhang,Qihua Feng,Zifeng Kang,Chaozhuo Li,Zhengxuan Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 比较式反馈（judge判断两个候选结果哪个更接近真实结果）配合Bradley-Terry目标训练世界模型时，并不必然学到真实结果概率，反而可能扭曲原本准确的分布：在模拟机器人推动任务中，模型把33–43个百分点的概率转移到任何枚举分支都不会产生的结果上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较式反馈是训练语言世界模型（为动作结果分配概率）的实用手段，但人们默认它能教会真实概率。本文检验这一假设，追问Bradley-Terry目标是否真的能恢复真实结果概率。

Method: 在带枚举结果分布的模拟机器人推动任务上，用Bradley-Terry目标拟合比较反馈。通过训练前解析计算目标最优解来预测模型收敛点；用已知judge规则的似然作为对照，在相同比较数据上训练；并在未见布局、五种候选定律、三倍更密枚举、以及剔除物理不可能事件等设定下检验偏移的稳健性，最后结合readout、response与decision trace做端到端审计。

Result: 原本在未见布局上准确（hazard误差2.4 pp）的模型，训练后把33–43 pp概率移到任何枚举分支都不产生的结果上。该偏移源于目标本身而非比较数据：最优解在训练前即可预测模型落点，而知晓judge规则的似然从同样比较中学习且不出现偏移。偏移稳健复现（未见条件、五种定律、三倍密枚举），剔除物理不可能事件后仍残留约29 pp。代价具条件性：含这些事件时抬高hazard概率，沿用反馈前阈值导致每决策约3 pp（已训练）与1–2 pp（未见条件）的效用损失（源于误拒）；剔除后六种子平均效用损失低于1 pp，但动作选择仍有小幅regret。

Conclusion: Bradley-Terry比较反馈的最优解与真实结果概率不一致，会系统性把概率质量推向枚举未覆盖的事件，且该偏移可预测、稳健、并经由概率传导到决策效用。因此比较式反馈不能默认作为概率校准手段，需与正确的judge概率模型或额外约束结合使用。

Abstract: Comparative feedback, in which a judge says which of two candidate outcomes is closer to what happened, is a practical way to train language world models that assign probabilities to an action's outcomes. We show that, fitted with a Bradley-Terry objective, it need not teach the true outcome probabilities and can distort accurate ones. On a simulated robot push with enumerated outcome distributions, this objective takes a model that is accurate on unseen layouts (hazard error 2.4 percentage points, pp) and moves 33 to 43 pp of its probability onto outcomes that none of a condition's enumerated branches produces. The cause is the objective, not the comparisons: its optimum, computed before training, predicts where the model ends up, and a likelihood that knows the judge's rule learns from the same comparisons without the shift. The shift is robust: it recurs on unseen conditions, under five candidate laws and a three-fold denser enumeration, and about 29 pp remain without the physically impossible events. Its cost is conditional: with those events it inflates hazard probabilities, and a threshold inherited from the pre-feedback model costs about 3 pp of utility per decision on trained and 1 to 2 pp on unseen conditions, through false rejects; without them the utility loss averages below 1 pp over six seeds, though a small regret in action choice remains. Audits of readout, response and decision trace the effect from probabilities to actions.

</details>


### [53] [Decision-Focused Reinforcement Learning for LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25597)
*Haotian Zhai,Leqi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出决策导向强化学习（DFRL），用下游决策后悔值作为奖励对LLM预测器做后训练，使预测直接服务于降低决策成本而非仅提升预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为预测器时，预测精度更高并不必然带来更低的决策成本；当预测值作为下游优化问题参数时，需直接以决策质量为训练目标。

Method: DFRL以负的下游决策后悔为奖励，对LLM预测器进行后训练，无需下游问题凸性或可微性，可嵌入现有RL后训练框架。为应对奖励评估昂贵，提出两种策略：(1) 启发式热启动，初期使用低成本近似奖励，后期切换为精确奖励精修；(2) 奖励校正，将低成本估计与随机精确评估结合，在保持策略梯度期望不变的同时减少每次更新的精确求解次数。

Result: 在三个合成优化问题上优于基于预测损失的基线；较大规模背包问题在短训练预算下早期提升更快；在基于H&M交易与商品描述的库存规划任务上，最佳配置（LLM先推理再预测）相较SFT基线降低后悔31.4%，相较最强传统预测基线降低12.4%。

Conclusion: 以决策后悔为奖励的后训练可使LLM预测器直接优化决策质量，且在奖励昂贵场景下可借近似奖励与校正机制保持梯度无偏、降低计算开销，实证有效。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise as predictors across various tasks, benefiting from their ability to use information from unstructured context. However, when predictions serve as parameters of downstream optimization problems, higher prediction accuracy does not necessarily translate into lower decision costs. We propose decision-focused reinforcement learning (DFRL), a framework for post-training LLMs to predict optimization parameters using negative downstream decision regret as reward. DFRL trains LLM predictors to minimize the costs of the decisions induced by their predictions, without requiring downstream convexity or differentiability, and integrates readily into existing RL post-training frameworks. To address expensive reward evaluation, we develop two approaches. Heuristic warm-starting uses inexpensive approximate rewards during an initial phase before switching to refinement with exact rewards. Reward correction combines inexpensive estimates with randomized exact evaluations, preserving the expected policy gradient while reducing the expected number of exact solves per update. Experiments on three synthetic optimization problems demonstrate improvements over prediction-loss baselines, while a larger knapsack experiment shows faster early improvement under short training budgets. On inventory planning with H&M transactions and product descriptions, our best configuration, in which the LLM reasons before predicting, reduces regret by 31.4% relative to the SFT baseline and 12.4% relative to the strongest evaluated conventional forecasting baseline.

</details>


### [54] [On the Informativeness of Structural Stability Bounds for GNNs: GINs versus GATs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25603)
*Alan Gany,Paul Landrier,Bogdan Cautis,Laks V. S. Lakshmanan,Silviu Maniu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文推导GIN与单头GATv1在非微小确定性边扰动下的结构稳定性界，并将其与一个与扰动无关的输出直径界作对比，指出GIN的稳定性界仅在满足"扰动规模-深度-最大度"条件时更紧，而GATv1的稳定性界永不变更紧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN稳定性界关注图变化时节点表示的漂移幅度，但很少与最简单的替代方案——任意两输出间距离的直径界（与图/扰动无关）——进行比较，因而难以判断稳定性界的实际价值。

Method: 针对GIN和单头GATv1，在允许非微小、确定性边扰动的前提下推导结构稳定性界；构造扰动无关的直径上界作为基线；对GIN给出稳定性界更紧的充要条件（联系扰动规模、深度与最大度），并在Cora与MUTAG上做实证检验。

Result: GIN：可精确刻画稳定性界更紧的条件，该条件在Cora上以较大裕度成立，而在低度图MUTAG上超过几层后即失效。GATv1：稳定性界从不比直径界更紧。

Conclusion: 稳定性界的有效性高度依赖图结构与深度；GATv1的分析需转向基于注意力质量（attention mass）的新框架。

Abstract: Stability bounds for graph neural networks limit how much node representations can drift when the graph changes, but they are rarely compared with the simplest alternative: a bound on the distance between any two outputs, whatever the graph. We derive structural stability bounds for Graph Isomorphism Networks (GINs) and single-head GATv1 networks under deterministic edge perturbations that need not be small, and compare each with such a perturbation-independent diameter bound. For GINs, we characterize exactly when the stability bound is tighter, through a condition linking perturbation size, depth and maximum degree; it holds with a wide margin on Cora, but fails beyond a few layers on the low-degree MUTAG graphs. For GATv1, the bound is never tighter, which calls for analyses based on attention masses.

</details>


### [55] [PSTO: A PDE-structured transport operator with shared implicit updates for multiscale CDR systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25607)
*Kaiwen Li,Dezhi Zhou,Kun Wu,Xingcai Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PDE-Structured Transport Operator (PSTO)，仅用无反应数据学习对流-扩散动力学，推理时无需重训练即可泛化到完整CDR系统；三个基准上100步耦合后平均相对L2误差较最强基线降低至少50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多尺度CDR系统耦合空间输运与局部刚性强反应动力学，数值模拟代价高；传统神经代理需昂贵的耦合CDR训练数据，且参数量随输运变量数增长，难以扩展到高维CDR系统。

Method: 将PDE结构嵌入神经架构，构建PSTO；所有输运变量共享单个输运算子块，并通过松弛不动点迭代在潜空间进行类隐式修正，提升递归多步预测稳定性且不增加可训练参数。

Result: 在规范2D CDR系统、多尺度Robertson反流火焰和多尺度氢气平面射流火焰三个基准上，PSTO在100步耦合后平均相对L2误差较最强神经基线降低至少50%；不动点迭代消融与误差分解验证其兼具高单步精度与长期稳定性；时间步敏感性分析显示时间步放大十倍仍保持精度，而基线精度迅速衰减。

Conclusion: PSTO通过将输运学习与局部动力学处理解耦，实现多尺度CDR动力学的高效模块化建模。

Abstract: Multiscale convection-diffusion-reaction (CDR) systems couple spatial transport with local stiff reaction kinetics, making their numerical simulation computationally demanding. Conventional neural surrogates require costly coupled CDR training data, and their parameter counts scale with the number of transported variables, limiting scalability to high-dimensional CDR systems. Here we propose a PDE-Structured Transport Operator (PSTO) that learns convection--diffusion dynamics solely from reaction-free data. By embedding the PDE structure into the neural architecture, PSTO generalizes to full CDR systems at inference without retraining. A single transport operator block is shared across all transported variables and refined through relaxed fixed-point iterations, performing an implicit-like correction in latent space. This improves stability during recursive multi-step prediction without adding trainable parameters. We evaluate PSTO on three benchmarks: a canonical 2D CDR system, a multiscale Robertson counterflow flame, and a multiscale hydrogen planar jet flame. In every case, PSTO reduces the mean relative $L_2$ error after 100 coupled steps by at least 50% compared to the strongest neural baseline. Ablation studies on the fixed-point iterations and detailed error decomposition further confirm that PSTO achieves both high one-step accuracy and long-term stability in coupled rollouts. Time-step sensitivity analysis shows that PSTO maintains accuracy even with a tenfold larger time step, whereas baseline accuracies decay rapidly as the time step increases. This framework enables efficient, modular modeling of multiscale CDR dynamics by separating transport learning from the treatment of local kinetics.

</details>


### [56] [Deviations from the NTK Regime in CNNs under Joint Depth-Width Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25624)
*Maria Matveev,Pascal Esser,Gitta Kutyniok,Mariia Seleznova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文数值研究CNN中深度L与宽度C的联合缩放对NTK集中性（初始化时的色散）与训练中核旋转的影响，发现L/C可能是控制NTK偏离无限宽极限的关键比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NTK的集中性和训练期恒定性取决于宽度、深度与初始化的相互作用；全连接、残差、循环网络的深度-宽度联合缩放已有研究，但CNN中相关效应尚不清楚，因此需要系统考察。

Method: 以卷积层数L为深度、每层通道数C为宽度，在不同初始化区域（有序、临界、混沌）下数值测量初始化时的NTK色散，以及训练过程中的核旋转（kernel rotation）。

Result: 在临界与混沌初始化下，NTK色散随L/C增大而增大，而在有序区保持较小；临界初始化时色散曲线近似随L/C塌缩，提示存在非平凡的联合缩放区。训练中核旋转总体随L/C增加，即便在初始色散很小的有序区也如此。

Conclusion: L/C在若干区域内可能主导NTK对无限宽极限的偏离；NTK在初始化时的集中性与训练时的稳定性呈现不同的缩放行为。

Abstract: The concentration and training-time constancy of the neural tangent kernel (NTK) depend on the interplay between network width, depth, and initialization. While joint depth-width scaling has been studied in fully-connected, residual, and recurrent networks, its effects in convolutional neural networks (CNNs) remain less understood. We investigate these effects numerically in CNNs, examining NTK dispersion at initialization and kernel rotation during training. Let depth $L$ be the number of convolutional layers, and width $C$ the number of channels per layer. We find that NTK dispersion increases with $L/C$ in the critical and chaotic initialization regimes, but remains small in the ordered regime. At critical initialization, dispersion curves approximately collapse as a function of $L/C$, suggesting a nontrivial joint scaling regime. During training, kernel rotation generally increases with $L/C$, even in the ordered regime where initial dispersion is small. Together, these results suggest that $L/C$ may govern NTK deviations from the infinite-width limit in certain regimes, and that NTK concentration at initialization and stability during training exhibit different scaling behaviors.

</details>


### [57] [QUBO-based feature selection for MRI undersampling pattern design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25625)
*Xiaoxin Huang,Masayuki Ohzeki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将笛卡尔MRI欠采样掩膜设计建模为特征子集选择问题，用MI/CMI估计加采样数惩罚构造QUBO模型，以模拟退火/量子退火求解，在fastMRI膝部数据上15%–40%采样率下零填充重建误差均优于四种常规采样方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规欠采样掩膜（如随机、等间距、变密度等）缺乏面向数据的信息论指导，作者希望把掩膜设计转化为可优化的特征子集选择问题，从而在给定采样率下选出信息量更高、冗余更低的采样线组合。

Method: 把每条采样线视为二值特征，用候选掩膜及其在训练集上的评估结果估计互信息（MI）与条件互信息（CMI）；将MI/CMI估计与采样数惩罚组合为二次无约束二值优化（QUBO）模型，并用模拟退火与量子退火求解，得到特征子集及对应采样掩膜。在fastMRI膝部幅度参考图像上做傅里叶欠采样、零填充重建仿真，训练/测试集按患者不重叠划分。

Result: 在15%至40%的六个目标采样率下，所提掩膜的零填充重建平均RMSE均低于四种常规采样方法；平均SSIM在所有测试采样率下均高于单变量互信息排序方法。

Conclusion: 在所测试的零填充仿真设定下，信息论特征选择可作为MRI欠采样掩膜设计的有效途径；结论受限于零填充重建与特定数据集/采样率范围。

Abstract: We formulate undersampling mask design for Cartesian magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) as a feature subset selection problem. Each sampling line is represented by a binary feature indicating whether it is included in each candidate mask. Using candidate masks and their training-set evaluations, we estimate mutual information (MI) and conditional mutual information (CMI). These estimates and a sampling-count penalty define a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) model. The model is solved using simulated annealing and quantum annealing to determine a feature subset and its corresponding sampling mask. We assess the selected subsets on the test set through zero-filled reconstruction in Fourier undersampling simulations using fastMRI knee magnitude reference images, with patient-disjoint training and test sets. At all six tested target sampling rates from 15% to 40%, the resulting reconstructions have lower mean root mean square error than those obtained with four conventional sampling methods. They also have higher mean structural similarity than those obtained using univariate mutual-information ranking at all tested rates. These results support information-theoretic feature selection as an approach to MRI undersampling mask design under the tested zero-filled simulation settings.

</details>


### [58] [NIMIS: A Structure-Agnostic, Chemically Informed Slot-Attention Framework for Demixing 1D $^1$H-NMR Mixture Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25629)
*Anna Marija Sumrova,Jonathan M. Goodman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NIMIS：一个结构无关的注意力深度学习框架，结合J耦合增强的多头Transformer与slot attention，将一维^1H-NMR混合物谱峰按来源组分分组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学反应产物多为复杂混合物，通常必须分离后才能获得可解释的^1H-NMR谱；直接分析粗混合物谱图可加速反应分析并拓展化学空间探索。

Method: NIMIS将J耦合信息直接注入多头Transformer编码器的注意力机制，得到语境化峰嵌入，再用slot attention将其分离为各组分集合；训练集为NMRexp数据库中实验谱构建的158,005个合成混合物，覆盖最多20个重原子、含C/H/N/O/S/F/Cl/Br/I的分子。

Result: 在留出分子上，峰到组分分配准确率为无噪82.28%、10%积分噪声77.34%；正确分配的平均NIMIS得分为0.83；优于常规Transformer与简单基线，且在骨架不相交评估中性能保持。

Conclusion: 通过将峰分组为组分级集合，NIMIS为更稳健的混合物分析方法（包括处理重叠信号的方法）奠定基础。

Abstract: The products of chemical reactions are usually complex mixtures that must be separated into their components before interpretable $^1$H-NMR spectra can be obtained. Direct analysis of the spectra of crude mixtures would accelerate reaction analysis and facilitate the exploration of new chemical space. We present NIMIS, a structure-agnostic, attention-based deep learning framework that groups the peaks of a 1D $^1$H-NMR mixture spectrum according to the component from which they originate. J-coupling information is introduced directly into the attention mechanism of a multi-head Transformer encoder, and the resulting contextualised peak embeddings are separated into components by slot attention. NIMIS was trained on 158,005 synthetic mixtures constructed from experimental spectra in the NMRexp database, covering molecules of up to 20 heavy atoms composed of C, H, N, O, S, F, Cl, Br and I. On held-out molecules it reaches a peak-to-component assignment accuracy of 82.28% for noise-free input and 77.34% with 10% integral noise, with a mean NIMIS score of 0.83 for correct assignments. NIMIS outperforms a conventional Transformer and simple baselines, and its performance carries over to a scaffold-disjoint evaluation. By grouping peaks into component-level sets, NIMIS provides a foundation for more robust mixture analysis methods, including methods capable of handling overlapping signals.

</details>


### [59] [Extending Rademacher Complexity-Based Generalization Theory to Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25650)
*Xi Wang,Liang Bai,Xian Yang,Richard Yi Da Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将域空间形式化为表示空间上的Wasserstein空间，基于其CD(K,∞)曲率理论推导出依赖几何结构的域泛化误差界，揭示了域泛化受表示空间的不变性与曲率共同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有域泛化理论引入带元分布的域空间以将泛化分析从样本级扩展到分布级，但通常将域空间视为无几何结构，缺乏对"分布级泛化"与"样本级泛化"差异的形式刻画，难以解释跨域泛化的内在困难。

Method: 将域空间形式化为表示空间上的Wasserstein空间，利用其几何性质（CD(K,∞)曲率理论）进行理论分析。

Result: 推导出一个依赖几何结构的域泛化误差界，通过引入曲率相关的置信项，形式化地解耦两个层级的泛化效应，并实验验证曲率对域泛化的影响。

Conclusion: 域泛化的本质受表示空间的不变性与曲率两方面共同影响，几何感知的理论框架弥补了无几何理论的根本局限。

Abstract: Domain generalization (DG) aims to train models on multiple source domains with distinct distributions to ensure robust performance on unseen target domains. In doing so, it elevates the concept of generalization from the traditional sample level to the distribution level. Existing theoretical frameworks introduce a domain space endowed with a meta-distribution to extend classical generalization analysis to this new setting. However, these approaches typically treat the domain space as devoid of geometry. In reality, the domain space is an inherently infinite-dimensional, nonlinear abstract space composed of probability distributions, and its complex geometric properties fundamentally determine generalization behavior. Consequently, existing geometry-free theories of domain generalization have a fundamental limitation. They lack a formal characterization of what distinguishes distribution-level generalization from sample-level generalization, leaving the inherent difficulty of generalizing across domains largely unexplained. To bridge this gap, we formalize the domain space as the Wasserstein space over the representation space. Building on the geometric properties of this space (specifically the $\mathrm{CD}(K,\infty)$ curvature theory) we derive a novel, geometry-dependent DG error bound. By introducing an additional curvature-dependent confidence term, this bound formally disentangles the two levels of generalization effects and reveals the essential nature that domain generalization is affected by both the invariance and the curvature of the representation space. Finally, we experimentally validate the effect of curvature on domain generalization.

</details>


### [60] [Explainability of genotype embedding models through observed cohort paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25681)
*Amashi Niwarthana,Aloysius Tan,Jagath C. Rajapakse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对基因型-神经影像融合模型，作者指出基于路径的归因（如Integrated Gradients）在基因型空间不成立，因其嵌入仅定义于离散等位基因剂量{0,1,2}；遂提出在嵌入空间中沿连接训练队列观测到的基因型路点的分段线性路径进行积分，保证完整性并提升归因忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 融合基因型与神经影像的深度学习模型预测神经疾病优于单一模态，但临床使用需要识别预测所依据的基因变异。Integrated Gradients等路径归因方法要求模型在从基线到输入的整条路径上都有定义，而基因型模型通过仅在{0,1,2}处定义的学习嵌入映射离散剂量，直线路径经过无嵌入、无梯度的分数基因型，从而违反该假设。

Method: 证明常用的逐变异估计器一般不满足完整性；加性可分性可保证完整性，但变异间交互会破坏该性质。提出在嵌入空间中沿分段线性路径（连接训练队列中观测到的基因型路点）进行积分，每条路径在连续意义上满足完整性，尽管段内部未必被观测到。

Result: 在两种扰动协议下，方法相对Integrated Gradients与离散化Integrated Gradients提升忠实度；在保持支撑集的扰动下于帕金森病分类取得最佳结果；在逐变异扰动下与support-background Shapley基线相当；在合成基准上具有更高种子稳定性并更好恢复植入的非加性效应。

Conclusion: 对离散基因型模型应在嵌入空间进行路径归因；基于观测路点的分段线性路径在保持完整性的同时显著提升忠实度与稳定性，为非加性遗传效应的可解释性提供可行方案。

Abstract: Deep learning models that combine genotypes with neuroimaging predict neurological diseases more accurately than either modality alone. Their clinical use, however, requires identifying which genetic variants a prediction is based on. Path-based attribution methods like Integrated Gradients address this by integrating the model's gradient from a baseline to the input. These methods assume the model accepts any point along that path as a valid input. Genotype models violate this assumption. They map the three discrete allele dosages \{0,1,2\} to vectors through a learned embedding defined only at those values, so a straight line passes through fractional genotypes at which no embedding, and therefore no gradient, exists. Attribution must therefore proceed in embedding space where the choice of path can affect the resulting attribution. We show that the commonly used per-variant estimator does not generally satisfy completeness. Additive separability guarantees completeness for this estimator, but interactions between variants generally break that property. Instead, we integrate along piecewise-linear embedding-space paths connecting genotype waypoints observed in the training cohort. Each path satisfies completeness in the continuum although the interiors of its segments are not necessarily observed. Our method improves faithfulness over Integrated Gradients and discretized Integrated Gradients under both perturbation protocols, achieves the strongest results for Parkinson's disease classification under support-preserving perturbations, and is competitive with a support-background Shapley baseline under per-variant perturbations. It also shows higher seed stability and better recovery of planted non-additive effects in a synthetic benchmark.

</details>


### [61] [Measurable Does Not Imply Transportable: A Prospective Audit of Model Explanations in Multi-Year Field Trials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25692)
*Rayhaneh Shabani Nia,Ali Karkehabadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出并实证检验"解释的前瞻可迁移性"(prospective explanation transportability)，用多年玉米田间试验证明：测量可靠性与上下文内解释可复现性，均不足以保证解释能在真实未来情境中重现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型解释日益被当作科学证据，但其数据之外的推断效力不明：当细粒度解释无法复现时，常被归因于目标测量噪声。由此引出核心问题——可靠测量是否意味着可复现的解释？现有工作混淆了特征重要性、测量可靠性与解释可迁移性。

Method: 形式化"前瞻解释可迁移性"这一独立性质；使用多年 Genomes-to-Fields 玉米试验，每个新年份作为真实未来情境而非随机重采样；在5个前向切分与1个前瞻锁定的2024测试年份上，评估16个农艺可解释的环境概念；比较粒度（环境级 vs 基因型条件级）与性状，并对照分半信度、上下文内重采样、以及非时序验证的偏差。

Result: 粗粒度环境级解释迁移可靠（中位余弦0.83），锁定2024值为0.811，落在预设80% bootstrap百分位区间内。籽粒产量的基因型条件解释历史中位余弦仅0.30（年份间0.006–0.822）。信度不能排序这些结果：分半信度最高的年份解释迁移最低。上下文内重采样显示GxE解释在固定情境内可复现（0.77–0.95），但跨2020–2023年际前瞻对齐低（0.01–0.36）。第二性状在两种粒度上均强迁移；非时序验证使交互级迁移被高估+0.22。

Conclusion: 目标可靠性与上下文内解释可复现性本身，并不能确定解释何时可支撑可复现的科学论断；解释的可迁移性是需要独立评估的维度，且非时序验证会系统性高估其稳健性。

Abstract: Model explanations are increasingly used as scientific evidence, yet it remains unclear what they license scientists to conclude beyond the data on which they were constructed. When a fine-grained explanation fails to replicate, the failure may be attributed to noisy measurement of its target, raising a fundamental question: does reliable measurement imply a reproducible explanation? We test this directly. We formalize prospective explanation transportability: the extent to which an explanation learned from past data is recovered in a genuinely future context. This property is distinct from feature importance and measurement reliability. Using multi-year Genomes-to-Fields maize trials, where each new year is a real future context rather than a random resample, we evaluate 16 agronomically interpretable environmental concepts across five forward splits and one prospectively locked test year. Coarse environment-level explanations transport reliably (median cosine 0.83); their locked 2024 value, 0.811, fell within a prespecified 80% bootstrap percentile interval. For grain yield, genotype-conditioned explanations have a historical median cosine of 0.30 and range from 0.006 to 0.822 across years. Reliability does not order these outcomes: the year with the highest split-half reliability had the lowest explanation transport. Within-context resampling showed that GxE explanations were reproducible within a fixed context (0.77-0.95) while exhibiting lower prospective alignment across the 2020-2023 transitions (0.01-0.36). A second phenotype transports strongly at both resolutions, and non-temporal validation inflates apparent interaction-level transport by +0.22. These results show that target reliability and within-context explanation reproducibility do not by themselves establish when a model explanation can support a reproducible scientific claim.

</details>


### [62] [PosteriorBench: Evaluating Behavior Posterior Sampling in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25735)
*Zhiyang Xun,Daniel D. Johnson,Sarah Schwettmann,Neil Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出 PosteriorBench 基准，用于衡量在给定语言模型与目标行为时，能否以可承受的计算代价从真实行为后验（给定行为下的转录分布）中高效采样，并对 21 个任务上的现有方法进行 KL 散度与计算成本对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的稀有行为难以研究，因为通过常规采样获得这些行为可能需要极大量的样本，因此需要高效的后验采样算法来获取指定行为的转录分布。

Method: 构建 PosteriorBench：基于 Qwen3.5-0.8B 设计 21 个任务，行为概率覆盖 10^-5 至 10^-1；利用十亿规模标注响应语料提供精确后验样本作为参照；以正向与反向 KL 散度衡量采样保真度，并统计计算成本，对提示法、后验样本微调及若干既有训练方法进行比较。

Result: 评估的方法均无法同时兼顾效率与保真：提示类简单方法成本低，但 KL 散度在高位饱和；在后验样本上微调往往计算代价过高；先前工作中的若干训练方法也未取得更优表现。

Conclusion: 构建能廉价近似真实后验的采样器仍是开放问题，PosteriorBench 可作为衡量该方向进展的基准，代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: Rare language model behaviors can be difficult to study because obtaining them through ordinary sampling can require a very large number of samples. This motivates the behavior posterior sampling problem: given a language model and a behavior of interest, is there an algorithm that efficiently samples from the model's distribution over transcripts that exhibit this behavior? We introduce PosteriorBench, a benchmark for comparing algorithms by their compute cost and by how close their samples are to the true posterior in forward and reverse KL divergence. PosteriorBench contains 21 tasks on Qwen3.5-0.8B with behavior probabilities from $10^{-5}$ to $10^{-1}$, each with exact posterior samples from a corpus of one billion labeled responses. We find that no method we evaluate is both efficient and faithful: simple methods like prompting are cheap but plateau at a high KL divergence from the posterior, fine-tuning on posterior samples is often cost-prohibitive, and several training methods from prior work do not perform better. Building a posterior sampler that can cheaply approximate the true posterior remains an open problem, and we hope PosteriorBench can measure progress towards this objective. Code and data are available at https://github.com/TransluceAI/PosteriorBench.

</details>


### [63] [Goal-Guided Reinforcement Learning via Fine-Tuning Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25742)
*Yajie Qi,Wei Wei,Lin Li,Lijun Zhang,Jianye Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CCGP-2M多模态目标偏好数据集与VLM引导的强化学习框架，用于Craftax稀疏奖励长时序环境下的探索，方法包含离线VLM微调（KLTDPO）与在线目标引导RL两阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Craftax类环境存在稀疏奖励与长时序问题，探索是RL智能体的主要挑战；直接用LLM生成高层目标缺乏任务适配，依赖精心设计的提示，易产生不一致或与上下文不符的目标。

Method: 构建Craftax-Classic交互轨迹的多模态目标偏好数据集CCGP-2M；框架分两阶段：离线阶段用KLTDPO（token级KL正则化结合分布鲁棒重加权）将VLM与目标偏好对齐；在线阶段进行目标引导的RL训练。

Result: 在Craftax-Classic上，方法比SOTA基线更早采集到钻石，取得最高分数与奖励，并在多数成就上获得最高成功率。

Conclusion: 学习环境特定的目标引导可有效提升稀疏奖励长时序环境中的探索效率与整体性能，VLM离线偏好对齐与在线目标引导RL的组合行之有效。

Abstract: Exploration remains a major challenge for reinforcement learning (RL) agents in Craftax-style environments due to sparse rewards and long horizons. Large language models (LLMs) can guide exploration by generating high-level goals, but methods using models without explicit task adaptation often rely on carefully designed prompts and may produce inconsistent or contextually inappropriate goals. We introduce CCGP-2M, a multimodal goal-preference dataset constructed from interaction trajectories in Craftax-Classic, and propose a vision-language model (VLM)-guided RL framework that learns environment-specific goal guidance from this dataset. The framework consists of two stages: offline VLM fine-tuning and online goal-guided RL. In the offline stage, we align the VLM with goal preferences using Kullback-Leibler Token-level Direct Preference Optimization (KLTDPO), which combines token-level KL regularization with distributionally robust reweighting. Experiments on Craftax-Classic show that our method collects diamonds earlier than state-of-the-art baselines, achieves the highest score and reward, and attains the highest success rates on most achievements.

</details>


### [64] [Disentangling Monitor Hardware Artifacts and Smoothing Lag for Shift-Robust Intraoperative EEG and Hemodynamic Risk Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25765)
*Zeyu Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出因果信号调理与迁移鲁棒的共形校准框架，处理麻醉监护设备伪迹，实现可靠的术中风险实时预测与不确定性界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用术中EEG与血压监护仪引入周期性阻抗脉冲、平滑延迟、直流漂移及袖带保持，干扰风险预测与不确定性校准，导致伪平线与深抑制混淆。

Method: 基于5,439例公开麻醉手术，构建流式滤波屏蔽10.13分钟64Hz阻抗检查、非截断陷波后调理、分离63秒抑制比滞后、区分三通道袖带充气与脱落；采用去阻抗皮层树集成对比深度序列模型，结合患者聚类子组协变量加权共形风险控制(CRC)与滞后缓冲在线共形PID跟踪。

Result: 预滤波屏蔽去除97.90%/97.60%假平线冲突并保留99.23%/98.96%深抑制；TIVA(N=525)上AUPRC 0.3135(1.94%流行率)与0.1627(0.82%无滞后)，与深度编码器无显著差异(p≥0.36)，低血压无皮层增益(p=0.784)；零重训练挥发转移(N=1,048)与呼气末气体标杆无显著差异(p=0.472)；CRC将全部70个TIVA迁移单元界在≤4.38%(α=5.0%)，每30秒片段66.32ms。

Conclusion: 因果伪迹解耦与迁移感知共形校准可在明确生理可观测性限制内支持可靠的实时术中风险界。

Abstract: Commercial intraoperative electroencephalography (EEG) and blood pressure monitors introduce cyclic impedance pulses, smoothing delays, direct-current (DC) drift, and cuff holds that confound risk forecasting and uncertainty calibration. We developed a causal signal-conditioning and shift-robust conformal calibration framework across 5,439 public anesthesia procedures. Streaming filters blank 10.13-min 64-Hz impedance checks, apply unclipped post-notch conditioning, separate 63-s suppression-ratio lag, and distinguish three-channel cuff inflations from dropouts across propofol-remifentanil total intravenous anesthesia (TIVA; N = 3,214), volatile anesthesia (N = 2,046), European endoscopy sedation (N = 171), and DC-coupled surgery (N = 8). De-impedanced cortical tree ensembles were compared against deep sequence models, paired with patient-cluster Subgroup-Covariate-Weighted Conformal Risk Control (CRC) and lag-buffered online Conformal Proportional-Integral-Derivative (PID) tracking. Pre-filter blanking removed 97.90% and 97.60% of false flatline conflicts while preserving 99.23% and 98.96% of deep suppression. On unseen TIVA surgeries (N = 525), cortical features achieved area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) 0.3135 (1.94% prevalence) and 0.1627 (0.82% lag-free prevalence), with no detectable difference from deep encoders (p >= 0.36) and no cortical gain for hypotension (0.3432 vs. 0.3437, p = 0.784). Zero-retraining volatile transfer (N = 1,048) showed no detectable difference from an end-tidal gas oracle on lag-free suppression (0.0820 vs. 0.0736, p = 0.472). Subgroup-Covariate-Weighted CRC bounded all 70 TIVA shift cells at <= 4.38% (alpha = 5.0%) in 66.32 ms per 30-s epoch. Causal artifact disentangling and shift-aware conformal calibration support reliable real-time intraoperative risk bounds within explicit physiological observability limits.

</details>


### [65] [RouteVerify: Routing Interventions for Step-Level Verification in Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25780)
*Yucheng Du,Ryan A. Rossi,Xiyang Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文用受控干预法（RouteVerify）检验"路由状态编码正确性"与"验证判断依赖路由正确性"之间的推断是否成立，发现二者仅部分成立：正确性可从路由读出、替换路由会改变判断，但改变很少随源步骤正确性而变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE路由状态能区分正确与错误推理步骤，且改变路由会改变步骤级验证判断，容易让人推断判断"遵循路由所编码的信息"。作者要检验这一推断是否成立。

Method: 提出RouteVerify控制干预研究：在保持被评估输入不变的前提下，用另一条解题轨迹的内部激活替换选定激活；在9个MoE检查点上比较三种干预接口（router logits、MoE输出、MoE后残差状态），变化替换所在层与token位置；以度量模型"偏好INCORRECT而非CORRECT"的验证margin为观测指标，并对比来自正确步与错误步的激活所引起的判断差异。

Result: 四项关联结果：(1) router logits探针在8/9检查点区分正确与错误步骤（AUROC 0.80–0.88）；(2) 跨全部路由层替换router logits在9/9检查点改变判断，且强于范数匹配的随机替换；(3) 该改变很少依赖源步骤正确性——单层下9/9检查点中错误步与正确步路由产生不可区分的margin，全层路由下仅1个检查点差异显著为正；(4) 局部残差替换在8/9检查点呈正向差异，说明该度量在存在依赖时确实能检测到。

Conclusion: 正确性可从路由中读出，路由替换也能移动验证判断，但判断的移动很少跟随正确性；提示路由编码与判断使用之间存在解耦。

Abstract: Routing states in mixture-of-experts (MoE) models can distinguish correct from erroneous reasoning steps. If changing routing also changes a model's step-level verification judgment, it is tempting to conclude that the judgment follows what routing encodes. We test this inference with RouteVerify, a controlled intervention study that replaces selected internal activations from one solution trace with activations from another, while keeping the evaluated input unchanged. Across nine MoE checkpoints, we compare three intervention interfaces: router logits, MoE outputs, and post-MoE residual states. We vary the layers and token positions at which activations are replaced. We measure changes in a verification margin that quantifies the model's preference for INCORRECT over CORRECT. We also evaluate the same input with activations from correct versus erroneous steps to test whether the resulting judgment depends on the source step's correctness. We report four linked results. First, probes on router logits separate correct from erroneous steps in eight checkpoints (AUROC 0.80-0.88). Second, replacing router logits across all routed layers changes the judgment in all nine checkpoints, and by more than a norm-matched random replacement. Third, this change rarely depends on whether the source step is correct. At one layer, routing from erroneous steps and from correct steps produces indistinguishable margins in all nine checkpoints. Under all-layer routing, their difference is resolved and positive in only one. Fourth, the same difference is positive for local residual replacements in eight of nine checkpoints, so the measure does detect such dependence where it exists. Correctness is therefore readable from routing, and routing replacements move the judgment, but the movement rarely follows correctness.

</details>


### [66] [How Reliable Are Explanations of Language Models That Detect Insider Threats?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25796)
*Suramya R. Angdembay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"临界半径"(tipping radius)指标,量化神经元解释对控制变量度量选择的脆弱性;在CERT内部威胁日志上审计语言模型检测器,33/34次运行中临界半径不超过两种常用度量间距的18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内部威胁检测器多采用不透明的神经网络,其解释仅指向内部神经元激活模式(哪些神经元在某天最活跃)。要检验这类解释是否真的支撑了flag分数,通常需要"移除特征"并与"相似但不移除"的控制条件做对比;而"相似"必须先选定一种距离度量,度量选择本身就成了未受控的自由度,作者希望量化结论对该选择的敏感程度。

Method: 在CERT模拟办公日志上训练七个语言模型检测器(共34次运行,含14次预注册主运行、开源模型)。检验方式:删除解释所指特征,观察检测器分数变化,并与尽可能相似但不删除特征的控制条件对比。核心贡献是tipping radius——对"度量需要移动多远,删除与控制的差异才会消失"的一阶预测,用以衡量结果的脆弱性;同时用同幅度随机扰动作为对照,并考察flag分数与工人身份的相关性、以及被命名特征随比较对象的变化。

Result: 33/34次运行中,临界半径不超过两种标准度量间距的18%;移动该距离可消除至少58%的差异甚至使差异反转,而同幅度随机移动基本不改变差异。结果在14次预注册主运行中的13次上复现,但在作者自训练的检测器上差异常小到无法测量。此外发现flag分数部分反映"工人是谁"而非"做了什么",且被点名的特征依赖于比较对象。

Conclusion: 神经元式解释的审计结论高度依赖控制条件的度量选择,属于脆弱结论。建议审计实践:预先固定控制条件的度量、报告其临界半径,并披露所采用的比较基准。

Abstract: Organizations monitor their workers' activity for insider threats, in which workers misuse their legitimate access, by copying confidential files before leaving. Detectors score how unusual each day of activity is, flagging the most unusual for an analyst to review. Prominent current approaches use opaque neural networks, so explanations of a flag point to internal features: patterns of neuron activity most active on days of misuse. We audit such explanations for language-model detectors trained on CERT's simulated workplace logs. To test an explanation, we see what happens to the detector's score when we remove its features inside the model. We compare this to a control: a change that is as similar as possible to the removal, but without actually removing the features. Because defining "similar" requires choosing a metric, our central contribution is the tipping radius: a first-order prediction of how far that metric must move before the difference between removal and control disappears, a measure of how fragile the result is to that choice. In 33 of 34 runs of seven language models, the tipping radius is at most 18% of the distance between two standard choices of metric. Moving that far removes at least 58% of the difference or reverses it, while random moves of the same size leave it largely intact. The result replicates in thirteen of fourteen pre-declared primary runs on openly released models, though on our trained detectors the difference is often too small to measure. We also find that a flag's score partly reflects who the worker is, not what they did, and that the features named depend on whom they are compared against. Audits should therefore fix the control's metric in advance, report its tipping radius, and disclose that comparison.

</details>


### [67] [A Sparse Low-Rank Biclique Decomposition for Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25826)
*Antoine Vialle,Sergei Gerasimov,Aref Einizade,Antonio Ortega,Fragkiskos D. Malliaros,Jhony H. Giraldo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出签名双团分解（SBD），将整数加权图的邻接矩阵分解为若干稀疏带符号二值秩一因子之和加稀疏残差，保持离散、可解释与稀疏性，并给出贪心算法及理论保证，在真实图上实现>10倍压缩和最高6倍SpMM加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典低秩+稀疏分解（如鲁棒PCA）把低秩分量视为稠密、稀疏分量视为噪声，这与图邻接矩阵的稀疏是结构性的特点不符；稠密低秩因子会破坏图的组合与计算性质。

Method: 将结构化分量建模为稀疏带符号二值秩一因子之和，外加稀疏带符号残差；形式化固定秩与贪心SBD目标，推导基于增益的贪心算法并给出理论保证。

Result: 在含噪图中成功恢复块结构；因子化表示支持高效稀疏矩阵乘法；真实图上压缩因子超10倍，相对cuSPARSE的SpMM加速最高6倍。

Conclusion: SBD为图数据提供了替代稠密低秩分解的实用方案，兼顾离散性、可解释性与计算效率。

Abstract: Low-rank plus sparse decompositions, such as robust PCA, typically model the low-rank component as dense and the sparse component as corruption. This viewpoint is poorly suited to graph adjacency matrices, where sparsity is structural and dense low-rank factors destroy the combinatorial and computational properties of the graph. We propose signed biclique decomposition (SBD), a sparse low-rank decomposition for integer-weighted graphs that represents the structured component as a sum of sparse signed binary rank-one factors, together with a sparse signed residual. The resulting representation remains discrete, interpretable, sparse, and compatible with standard linear algebra. We formulate fixed-rank and greedy SBD objectives and derive a gain-based greedy algorithm with theoretical guarantees. Empirically, SBD recovers block structure in noisy graphs, while its factorized representation enables efficient sparse matrix-matrix multiplication (SpMM). On real graphs with strong shared-neighborhood structure, SBD achieves compression factors above $10$ and SpMM speedups up to $6\times$ over cuSPARSE, suggesting a practical alternative to dense low-rank decompositions for graphs. Code is available at https://github.com/IAntoineV/sbd.

</details>


### [68] [Beyond Alignment: Whitening Geometry, Profile Synthesis, and Heterogeneous Ensembles for Cross-Environment WiFi Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25831)
*Lei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出WiFi感知跨环境部署的失效源于编码器对新颖环境的泛化（外推）失败而非域对齐问题，并通过预注册的反证链否定主流对齐方法，转而提出组合式框架Dec-H，在K10设定下取得0.560（较基线+15.2pp）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实用WiFi感知的核心障碍是跨环境部署：训练环境top-1可达0.89+，但在仅K-shot标签的LODO评估下骤降至0.408。作者希望厘清该崩塌的真正成因，以找到有效改进杠杆。

Method: 首先做上限分析与预注册反证链：验证动作信号强度、环境身份可解码性（0.965）并证明"对齐域"类方法（训练时对齐、readout级原型对齐、对抗混淆、CORAL、子空间对齐）均带来负收益。随后提出Dec-H，整合四个有效杠杆：(i)闭式源域类内白化（等价于重参数化readout的L2正则）；(ii)profile-mixing增强；(iii)交替白化重拟合的少样本自适应；(iv)异构决策集成。

Result: 各组件增益分别为+8.6pp（白化，零成本）、+1.0pp（profile-mixing）、+1.5pp（少样本自适应，固定预条件器20/20均为负）、+1.7pp（异构集成）；Dec-H在K10达0.560、K40达0.812，较基线+15.2pp，在四个环境和多种子下一致。同质化扩展与源加权堆叠饱和或有害。

Conclusion: 跨环境WiFi感知的瓶颈是编码器外推而非域对齐；源域统计量与跨环境贡献呈反相关，因此有效手段在于几何重参数化、数据增强、自适应预条件与异构集成。全部十三个配置均预注册并冻结判据，结论可靠性较高。

Abstract: Cross-environment deployment is the central obstacle to practical WiFi sensing: a pipeline reaching 0.89+ top-1 in its training environments collapses to 0.408 under Leave-One-Domain-Out (LODO) evaluation with only K-shot labels.
