Evaluating the third ability requires reliably executing an experiment that an agent designs, with readings grounded in a real laboratory. We introduce TwinLab-Bench, which evaluates AI scientists on self-designed experiments in 52 tasks from laboratories in the physical sciences, the life sciences, and engineering. The benchmark supports more than 100 experimental action types, and the agent's report is scored by comparison with a hidden answer taken from real laboratory records. Each task has its own TwinLab agent, an agent that serves as the environment and executes the experiments that the tested agent designs, reading the raw materials of the laboratory and calling calculators that are built from its records. Compared with a language model that sees only the task description, the TwinLab agent almost eliminates readings that deviate from the real records and inconsistent answers to a repeated request, and closed-book probes control for answers recalled from memory. We find that these tasks remain hard for frontier models: the strongest, Claude Fable 5.2, averages a score of only 45.0, and we conduct an empirical study of where it fails.

</details>


### [205] [Similarity-based OSF Logic for Flexible SPARQL Query Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26261)
*Gian Carlo Milanese,Gabriella Pasi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了fosf推理器的新版本，在模糊OSF逻辑基础上新增相似性推理与OSF到SPARQL的翻译引擎，实现面向RDF知识图谱的柔性查询问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RDF知识图谱上的查询常因与本体不一致而无法返回结果，需要一种能利用模糊子类关系和相似关系进行近似匹配的柔性问答机制。

Method: 以模糊OSF逻辑（特征符号表函数、sort符号表模糊集、模糊subsumption关系及统一演算）为理论基础；新版本将OSF语言扩展为包含sort间相似关系，并加入把OSF项编译为标准SPARQL的翻译引擎；OSF查询在翻译前先经模糊本体感知重写，通过模糊OSF约束归一化融合sort定义理论、特征domain/range声明、模糊subsumption与相似关系。

Result: 实现了基于相似性的推理，并使以OSF项表达的查询能在重写后翻译为SPARQL，从而对原本与本体不一致的查询返回近似解。

Conclusion: fosf新版本将模糊OSF推理与SPARQL查询结合，支持灵活、本体感知的近似查询问答。

Abstract: We present an updated version of fosf, a reasoner for fuzzy order-sorted feature (OSF) logic, with support for flexible SPARQL query answering over RDF knowledge graphs. Fuzzy OSF logic is a knowledge representation and reasoning language where feature symbols denote functions and sort symbols denote fuzzy sets. Sorts are organized in a fuzzy subsumption relation that extends to OSF terms, record-like structures representing classes of entities. The unification algorithm for these structures provides a calculus of fuzzy type subsumption. The latest release of fosf introduces support for similarity-based reasoning by extending the OSF language with a similarity relation among sorts, alongside a translation engine for compiling OSF terms into standard SPARQL. Queries expressed as OSF terms undergo fuzzy ontology-aware rewriting prior to translation. This process incorporates an OSF theory of sort definitions, feature domain and range declarations, and fuzzy subsumption and similarity relations through fuzzy OSF constraint normalization. This rewriting enables flexible query answering by returning approximate solutions, allowing queries that would otherwise be inconsistent with the underlying ontology to yield relevant results.

</details>


### [206] [Client Exits - A Way to Increase LLM Transparency on the Client Side](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26309)
*Marc Jansen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Client Exits 机制：模型在输出中插入预定义的机器可解析标记，由客户端决定是否及如何将附加信息渲染为图形、文本、语音等模态，从而将语义意图与客户端呈现解耦；基于微调 Qwen 与改造 LibreChat 的原型验证了标记生成的可行性与偏好优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 生成的文本推荐内容难以检查，且附加信息的呈现方式被界面强绑定，缺乏在语义与展示之间分离的轻量机制。

Method: 模型在输出中插入预定义、机器可解释的标记；接收端客户端自行决定是否及如何实现对应增强。采用 Question–Option–Criteria（QOC）方法，设计三种基于 XML 的 client exits；用几乎相同的训练样例训练两个 QOC 变体，仅其中一个加入 client-exit 标注。实现为微调 Qwen 模型 + 改造 LibreChat 客户端的原型。

Result: 在 100 个测试问题上，client-exit 模型在每个回答中均包含全部三种标记；在未被告知偏好该机制的 LLM 评审下，该模型回答在 66% 的对比中被偏好。

Conclusion: 标记生成在评测集上成功实现，验证了客户端可控增强（client-controlled enrichment）的可行性；但评估范围限于标记生成与偏好比较，尚未覆盖真实用户交互与多模态呈现效果。

Abstract: Textual recommendations generated by large language models (LLMs) can be difficult to inspect. This paper introduces \emph{Client Exits}, a lightweight mechanism for enriching LLM responses while separating semantic intent from client-specific presentation. The model inserts predefined, machine-interpretable markers into its output, and the receiving client decides whether and how to realize the corresponding enrichments. This allows supplementary information to be presented as graphics, text, speech, or other modalities without prescribing a concrete interface. We implement a prototype combining a finetuned Qwen model with a modified LibreChat client. Using the Question--Option--Criteria (QOC) approach including three XML-based client exits. We evaluate two QOC-oriented model variants trained on essentially the same examples, with client-exit annotations added for one variant. Across 100 test questions, the client-exit model includes all three markers in every response. An LLM judge, informed about the mechanism without being instructed to favor it, prefers this model's answers in 66\% of the comparisons. These results demonstrate successful marker generation on the evaluated test set and support the feasibility of client-controlled enrichment.

</details>


### [207] [Actor-Predictor Architecture for Reinforcement Learning with Formal Specification Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26313)
*Josef Brozovich,Qining Zhang,Lei Ying*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Actor-Predictor 框架，用学习到的"逻辑进展"代理表示替代显式自动机构造，使强化学习能满足形式化时序安全约束，并扩展到非正则规约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将逻辑公式转为自动机做状态增强与奖励塑形的方法，前提是自动机构造可行且可处理，因而局限于小型规约和线性时序逻辑（LTL），难以适用于复杂或非正则规约。

Method: 训练一个预测器持续估计"部分轨迹在未来是否将满足形式化规约"，免去显式自动机构造；将其学到的特征作为辅助输入提供给 actor，并用 GRPO 与 RWR 优化策略。理论上证明：在预测精度充分时，所学表示与自动机状态等价。

Result: 在网格世界与 Isaac Lab 机器人任务上，Actor-Predictor 在 LTL 约束任务中优于基于自动机的基线；在有限自动机方法不适用的非正则规约（如 a^n b^n）上同样表现良好。

Conclusion: 以可学习的预测式代理表示替代自动机，是一种更具可扩展性与通用性的时序安全 RL 方案，可突破自动机构造对规约类型与规模的限制。

Abstract: One fundamental challenge in reinforcement learning (RL) is enforcing temporal safety constraints, such as satisfying formal specifications. Existing methods often transform logic formulas into automata for state augmentation and reward shaping. However, this approach assumes that automaton construction is both feasible and tractable, which limits its applicability to small specifications and linear temporal logic (LTL). This paper proposes Actor-Predictor, a framework that learns a surrogate representation of logic progress without explicitly constructing an automaton. The predictor continuously estimates whether a partial trajectory will satisfy the formal specification in a later step, and its learned features are provided as auxiliary inputs to the actor, which is then optimized using GRPO and RWR. We show theoretically that, under sufficient predictive accuracy, the learned representation is equivalent to the automaton state. Experiments on grid world and robotic tasks in Isaac Lab demonstrate that Actor-Predictor outperforms automaton-based baselines on LTL-constrained tasks. It also performs well on non-regular formal specifications, such as $(a^n b^n)$, for which finite-automaton-based approaches are not applicable.

</details>


### [208] [Rigel-Ladon: An Option-Count Law for a One-Pass Decision Encoder, Tested on Held-Out Option Counts and Held-Out Schemas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26332)
*Mohammed Nayeem Khan,Vrishank Tiwari,Navin Kumar R*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Laya 单次前向即可给出 K 个选项的概率；其 logit 准确率随 log(K-1) 线性下降，拟合小 K 可外推至中等 K，但在 35 标签池上失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多选项阅读理解中，选项数 K 从 2 增至 35 时模型准确率如何衰减，以及这种衰减是否可被简单定律预测、能否迁移到分布外题型。

Method: 在四个问题池上构造嵌套选项集（每个 K 选项问题包含更小 K 的子集），对 Base 与微调模型 FT 打分并逐一与单个对手比对；FT 在 12 选项同族（话题）问题上训练，12 选项 HR（部门路由）问题为异族。以 logit 准确率（正确与错误答案 logit 之差的对数）对 log(K-1) 做线性拟合，检验外推能力，并评估 IIA、顺序平均、短名单、温度律与校准预测集。

Result: logit 准确率对 log(K-1) 呈线性，斜率 0.68–0.88；仅用 K≤6 拟合即可将 K=7–12 预测在 0.6 分内、K=24 在 1.2 分内（对数线性规则为 1.6/6.7）。用话题问题斜率加两项准确率即可预测 HR 其余 K，误差 0.3 分（FT）与 0.7 分（Base）。35 标签五模式池上该定律失效（5–11 分）。IIA 被拒绝：金银与对手的 log-odds 每翻倍 K 偏移 +0.24 至 +2.07 nats，但成对答案仍可在 4.7 分内复现 K 路准确率。顺序平均、短名单、温度律与校准预测集均未迁移（FT 的 90% 预测集在 HR 上仅覆盖 84%）。预先做出的 10 项预测中 2 项成立。

Conclusion: 选项数-准确率呈 log-linear 衰减，且斜率可跨题型迁移，为小 K 校准外推提供可行工具；但该定律在大标签池失效，IIA 不成立，多数加性/校准技巧不可迁移。局限：单一模型族与 LLM 标注的合成文本。

Abstract: Laya is a small neural network that reads a text, a question and K written options, and returns one probability per option in a single pass. We ask how its accuracy falls as K grows from 2 to 35 and whether that can be predicted. We score nested option sets (every K-option question contains the smaller ones) on four question pools, for the released model (Base) and our fine-tune (FT), and score every item against each single rival. FT trained on one 12-option topic question (in-family); a 12-option HR (department-routing) question is out-of-family. Logit accuracy (the log of correct over incorrect answers) is linear in log(K-1) with slope 0.68 to 0.88. Fitted on K up to 6, the law predicts K=7 to 12 within 0.6 points and K up to 24 within 1.2 (a log-linear rule: 1.6 and 6.7). Taking the slope from the topic question and measuring only two-option accuracy on HR predicts its other K within 0.3 points (FT) and 0.7 (Base). On a 35-label pool of five schemas the law fails (5 to 11 points). Independence of irrelevant alternatives is rejected: the gold-versus-rival log-odds shifts by +0.24 to +2.07 nats per doubling of K, yet pairwise answers reproduce K-way accuracy within 4.7 points. Order averaging (averaging over several orderings of the options), shortlists, a temperature law and calibrated prediction sets did not transfer (FT's 90% sets cover 84% on HR). 2 of 10 predictions made in advance held. Caveats: one model family and LLM-labelled synthetic text.

</details>


### [209] [ClosureBench: A Benchmark for Reasoning under Closure Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26335)
*Francesco Belardinelli,Flavio Corradini,Matteo Leonesi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ClosureBench 基准，用于测试 LLM 智能体是否遵循封闭世界/开放世界等"缺失信息处理规则"，发现总体准确率会掩盖大量封闭假设切换错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测无法区分"信息缺失被当作未知"与"被当作假"这两种情形，因为这类判断往往与其他推理需求交织在一起，导致无法单独评估模型对缺失信息的处理能力。

Method: 构建 ClosureBench：每个基础场景在 OWA（开放世界假设）、CWA（封闭世界假设）、LCWA（局部封闭世界假设）三种变体下各测一次，变体间仅改变对缺失信息的处理方式；模型须在所有变体上全部答对才算在基础场景上正确。另提供智能体扩展，覆盖动作选择、信息源范围与知识更新。共 960 个实例。

Result: 重复运行下总体准确率为 71.18%–99.86%，但在金标准标签真正随契约变化的核心切换场景上，准确率仅 18.23%–99.31%；LCWA 下差距尤为明显，部分智能体在提示已将缺失信息规定为假的情况下仍回答"未知"。

Conclusion: 对 AI 智能体的评测不能只考察其能否对已有事实进行推理，还应考察其在信息缺失时是否采用正确的假设；总体准确率会严重掩盖契约遵循层面的缺陷。

Abstract: AI agents built on large language models (LLMs) often answer questions from incomplete records. Missing information might be interpreted as unknown or false, depending on the completeness of the source. Current evaluations fail to distinguish these two aspects, as they are usually interwoven with other reasoning demands. We present ClosureBench, a benchmark that tests whether LLMs follow the stated rules for dealing with missing information. Every base scenario is tested under OWA, CWA, and LCWA, and only the treatment of missing information changes between variants. ClosureBench provides a verified base benchmark as well as agent extensions for action choice, source scope, and updates. A model is considered correct on a base scenario only if it provides correct answers in all of its closure variants. Across 960 instances, overall accuracy can mask substantial closure mistakes. Models achieve overall accuracy from 71.18% to 99.86% across replicated runs, but their core switch accuracy, measured on the scenarios whose gold label actually changes with the contract, varies between 18.23% and 99.31%. Large discrepancies emerge under LCWA. For example, some agents choose unknown despite the prompt indicating that missing information should be treated as false. Our findings demonstrate that evaluations of AI agents should not only measure whether agents can reason over existing facts but also whether they use the appropriate assumption when information is absent.

</details>


### [210] [Sirius-Tiamat: How Small Can a One-Pass Decision Encoder Get Before Its Decisions Change?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26339)
*Mohammed Nayeem Khan,Vrishank Tiwari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 系统测绘 421M 一次性决策编码器 Laya 的压缩极限：bf16/6-bit/剪枝/蒸馏的翻转率与校准代价，域外远比同域敏感，等存储下量化优于剪枝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析一次性决策编码器（Laya，421M 参数）压缩到何种程度会导致判定翻转，并量化不同压缩手段对决策一致性与校准性的影响。

Method: 以 Laya 自身 fp32 输出为基准，统计决策翻转率并测量 ECE；在 1,221 条同 schema 合成样本、273 条未见 schema 样本与 800 条 ARC/MMLU/HellaSwag 域外问题上，比较 bf16、6/5-bit 分组量化、层剪枝（删 6 层）、逐张量单 scale 量化、CPU dynamic int8、早退机制以及 16 层蒸馏学生模型；并引入单温度重校准与重训练。

Result: bf16 同域翻转 0.2%、域外 2.2%；6-bit 为 1.3%/5.9%；删 6 层为 0.9%/9.1%，ECE 由 5.9% 升至 39.2%，单温度（无需标签）可回落至 3.6%。逐张量单 scale 在低于 6-bit 时失效；CPU dynamic int8 翻转达 30%，几乎集中于单一层。TV 距离对舍入与翻转一致，对剪枝不一致；早退需先重校准置信门控才能优于静态裁剪。16 层蒸馏学生同域翻转由 44% 降至 9%（域外 45%）。等存储预算下舍入优于剪枝。

Conclusion: 压缩对域外决策的破坏显著大于同域；校准误差主要由剪枝引起且可低成本修复；等存储成本下量化舍入比层删除更保真；结论限于合成/LLM 标注数据与单一架构。

Abstract: A one-pass decision encoder reads a text, a question and several named options, and returns a probability for each option in a single forward pass. We ask how far Laya, a 421M-parameter encoder of this kind, can be compressed before its decisions change. We count decisions that differ from Laya's own full-precision (fp32) answer, and track calibration (how well confidence matches accuracy, measured by expected calibration error, ECE), on 1,221 synthetic items with the training schemas, 273 with an unseen schema, and 800 out-of-family questions from ARC, MMLU and HellaSwag. Layer pruning of a related model family is known; we add flips, calibration, held-out data and retraining. bf16 changes 0.2% of in-family decisions but 2.2% out of family; 6-bit grouped rounding 1.3% and 5.9%; deleting 6 layers 0.9% and 9.1%, while ECE rises from 5.9% to 39.2%. One fitted temperature, needing no labels, restores it to 3.6%. One scale per tensor fails below six bits, and CPU dynamic int8 changes 30% of decisions, nearly all through one layer. Total variation tracks flips for rounding but not for deletion, and an early exit saves layers over a static cut only if its confidence gate is first recalibrated. Distilling a 16-layer student cuts its in-family flips from 44% to 9% (45% out of family). At equal stored size, rounding flips fewer decisions than deletion. Data are synthetic and LLM-labelled, for one architecture.

</details>


### [211] [Training LLMs to Follow Reasoning Instructions with Minimal Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26342)
*Alexandra Yakovleva,Luat Gia Khoi Nguyen,Juho Kannala,Harri Valpola,Alexander Ilin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"基于编辑的训练"（edit-based training）框架：只对模型自身轨迹中"首次失败点"的最小替换片段进行监督，从而将监督信号集中在推理真正出错之处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有塑造长链推理行为的训练信号过于粗糙：全轨迹监督微调要求模型模仿整段示范，过度约束输出分布；轨迹级强化学习只有轨迹级奖励，信用分配弱，无法定位成功或失败的具体位置。

Method: 给定采样得到的completion，由编辑者在指定准则下定位首个失败点并提出最小替换span，模型仅在这些被编辑的span上接受监督。方法可离线使用也可在线使用，在线模式持续将监督适配到模型不断变化的失败模式。在ReasonIF基准上以Qwen3-32B实例化，用于推理指令遵循任务。

Result: 与全轨迹模仿和轨迹级强化学习基线相比，基于编辑的训练显著提升了对推理指令的遵循程度，同时基本保持了模型在非目标任务上的能力。

Conclusion: 局部化纠错是塑造LLM推理方式的有效补充手段，可与全轨迹SFT和轨迹级RL互补。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly rely on extended reasoning traces, yet the training signals used to shape this behavior remain coarse. Supervised fine-tuning on full trajectories forces the model to imitate an entire demonstration, overconstraining its output distribution, while trajectory-level reinforcement learning provides only weak credit assignment, rewarding or penalizing whole completions without identifying where they succeed or fail. We propose edit-based training, a general framework for learning from a model's own trajectories via localized textual corrections. Given a sampled completion, an editor identifies the first point of failure under a specified criterion and proposes a minimal replacement span. The model is then supervised only on these edited spans. By design, this concentrates supervision where the model's reasoning first breaks down and aims to preserve its existing reasoning patterns elsewhere. The method applies both offline and online, with the latter continually adapting supervision to the model's evolving failure modes. We instantiate this approach on reasoning instruction following with Qwen3-32B on the ReasonIF benchmark. Compared to full-trajectory imitation and trajectory-level reinforcement learning baselines, edit-based training substantially improves compliance with the reasoning instruction while largely preserving the model's skills on untargeted tasks. These results suggest that localized correction is an effective complement to these approaches for shaping how LLMs reason.

</details>


### [212] [DeepQuest: From Knowledge Gaps to Scientific Discovery in Alzheimer's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26343)
*Chunbo Jiang,Yifei Chen,Beining Wu,Fuyou Mao,Weihong Chen,Enyu Bao,Mingxuan Liu,Feiwei Qin,Qiyuan Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepQuest是一个以知识缺口为驱动的AI科学家框架，用于阿尔茨海默病研究，通过两级审计实现可追溯、有证据支持的科学发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化实验并不必然推进科学理解；研究智能体需识别有意义的知识缺口、判断现有数据能否解决，并评估发现是否推进既有知识，现有框架缺乏对此的系统对齐与审计。

Method: 将领域综述中的未解决问题转化为可追溯、可检验的假设，经数据可行性筛选与分组Elo排序确定优先级；迭代实验与两级审计相结合，将实验可靠性与相对两个冻结文献语料的知���贡献分开评估。

Result: 在OASIS-3上产生42个可行假设，在三位语言模型评审的15个评估维度中有14项优于最强基线；整体框架审计判定的科学发现率达95.24%（优于通用执行器与领域专用研究智能体），且在不同文献语料与评审模型族下保持稳定；代表性发现（神经精神症状与痴呆风险、淀粉样蛋白空间纵向稳定性、新皮层tau相关临床损害）在独立ADNI与AIBL队列中得到支持。

Conclusion: 对齐知识缺口、数据可行性与证据审计，可将自动化研究转化为可追溯、有证据支持的科学发现。

Abstract: Automating experiments does not necessarily advance scientific understanding. Research agents must identify meaningful knowledge gaps, determine what available data can resolve, and assess whether their findings advance existing knowledge. We introduce DeepQuest, a knowledge-gap-driven AI scientist framework for Alzheimer's disease research. DeepQuest transforms unresolved questions from domain reviews into traceable, testable hypotheses and prioritizes them through data feasibility screening and groupwise Elo ranking. It couples iterative experimentation with two-level auditing that separates experimental reliability from knowledge contribution relative to two frozen literature corpora. On OASIS-3, DeepQuest produces 42 feasible hypotheses and outperforms the strongest baselines in 14 of 15 evaluator-dimension comparisons across three language-model reviewers. The full framework achieves an audit-assessed scientific finding rate of 95.24%, outperforming both general-purpose executors and domain-specific research agents. This scientific finding rate remains stable across literature corpora and evaluator model families. Representative findings concern neuropsychiatric symptoms and dementia risk, longitudinal amyloid spatial stability, and neocortical tau-associated clinical impairment, with key associations supported in independent ADNI and AIBL cohorts. These results show how aligning knowledge gaps, data feasibility, and evidence auditing can turn automated research into traceable, evidence-supported scientific discovery. Our source code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DeepQuest-3066/

</details>


### [213] [Context as a Resource](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26346)
*Xufei Tian,Han Hu,Wenli Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出无路由架构 ClonAct，用可插拔单遍非生成式上下文 MMU 直接对上下文块做虚拟化，在多流基准上以更少 token 达到真冲突合并上限并保持低等待。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨会话并发任务消耗人类注意力，亟需单一连续界面下的常驻个人智能体；但共享冻结主干权重时，交错对话与外部事件涌入单一线性会话会导致跨主题约束渗漏与队头阻塞，而路由-票据式层级又带来有损文本交接、路由开销大与盲目合并（真冲突合并仅 33.3%）。

Method: ClonAct：无路由架构，通过可插拔的单遍非生成式 Context MMU 虚拟化上下文块，配合就地 Context Cells、带隔离试合并门（trial merge gate）的足迹感知 Synaptic Bus，以及波冲刷式 Shared Trunk（波次刷新共享主干）。不做拟人化角色调度。

Result: 在三个程序化多流基准、三个模型家族上：可移植试合并门将真冲突合并提升至 63.3%；ClonAct 的无路由虚拟化在该上限下减少 45.9% 提示 token，并避免小模型路由崩塌；持续激增流下达到最高完成率 95.0%，中位等待最低，超时状态检查为零。

Conclusion: 直接虚拟化上下文块优于角色调度与路由票据层级，可在同等合并在上限下显著降低 token 与延迟开销，并在小模型与高负载流下保持稳定。

Abstract: Managing concurrent tasks across separate agent sessions quickly exhausts human attention, motivating always-on personal agents behind a single continuous interface. Yet when every context shares identical frozen backbone weights, funneling interleaved dialogues and external events into one linear session induces cross-topic constraint bleed and head-of-line stalling. Conversely, router-ticket hierarchies incur lossy prose handoffs, heavy routing overhead, and blind merges - collapsing true-conflict repository merges to a 33.3% three-run mean despite high isolated branch passes. Rather than scheduling anthropomorphic roles, we introduce ClonAct, a routerless architecture that virtualizes context blocks directly through a pluggable single-pass non-generative Context MMU, in-place Context Cells, a footprint-aware Synaptic Bus with an isolated trial merge gate, and a wave-flushed Shared Trunk. Across three programmatic multi-stream benchmarks and three model families, our portable trial merge gate lifts true-conflict merges to 63.3%, while ClonAct's routerless virtualization attains this ceiling with 45.9% fewer prompt tokens and prevents compact-model router collapse. Under continuous surge streams, ClonAct matches top completion (95.0%) with the lowest median wait and zero timed-out status checks.

</details>


### [214] [How Much Should an Agent Remember? Deployment Lessons from Self-Distilled Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26361)
*K. R. Jayaram,Vatche Isahagian,Aswath Vaithinathan Aravindan,Vinod Muthusamy,Punleuk Oum,Gaodan Fang,Michal Ulewicz,Gegi Thomas,Evelyn Duesterwald*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出从智能体自身轨迹中挖掘"lessons"并整合为"guidelines"的自我经验学习流水线（已开源并落地于IBM CUGA与watsonx Orchestrate），核心发现是"合适的内存规模取决于模型而非记忆本身"：强模型随guideline增多持续受益，弱模型则在小规模子集上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程任务中LLM智能体以重复性、程序化的方式失败（如API分页错误、实体解析错误、回答未被提问的问题）。作者希望让智能体从自身历史轨迹中学习，并把该能力工程化、产品化，同时记录构建与评测过程中的经验教训。

Method: 构建一条流水线：先从智能体自身轨迹中挖掘每回合1–3条简短"lessons"；再将其整合为更少的"guidelines"，并记录每条guideline合并了多少lessons（保留provenance）；推理时向智能体注入全部或经筛选的子集。以AppWorld为主评测基准，覆盖基线完成率40%–92%的多个模型；并报告失败案例与经验（高支持度guideline反噬、整合中provenance丢失、被吞掉的API错误、基线混淆）。

Result: DeepSeek-V3.2使用全部191条guidelines时任务完成率+9.5个百分点、场景完成率+16.1个百分点；gpt-oss-120b基线39.9%，使用全部199条达51.2%，但约29条的筛选子集达55.4%（两次运行均值）。

Conclusion: 记忆的最优规模是模型属性而非记忆属性：强模型受益于全量guidelines，弱模型更适合精选小子集；同时总结了provenance管理、错误可见性、基线对照与部署实践等方面的建议，为部署智能体记忆的团队提供参考。

Abstract: LLM agents on long-horizon tasks fail in recurring, procedural ways. They mis-paginate an API, resolve the wrong entity, or answer when no answer was asked. We built a pipeline that lets an agent learn from its own history. It first mines short \emph{lessons} from the agent's own trajectories, one to three per episode. It then consolidates those lessons into a smaller set of \emph{guidelines}, where each guideline records how many lessons it merged. At inference the agent receives either all guidelines or a selected subset. The pipeline ships in the open-source altk-evolve library and is deployed in IBM's CUGA agent and watsonx Orchestrate platform. We report what we learned building, measuring, and productizing it. Our primary instrument is the AppWorld benchmark, across models spanning 40--92\% baseline task completion. The central finding is that the right amount of memory is a property of the model, not the memory. A strong model keeps improving as guidelines are added. DeepSeek-V3.2 gains +9.5 points of task completion and +16.1 points of scenario completion with its full 191-guideline set. A weak model gains less from the same full set than from a small selected subset. gpt-oss-120b starts at 39.9\% task completion. It reaches 51.2\% with all 199 of its guidelines but 55.4\% (mean of two runs) with a selected subset of about 29. Around that result we report what went wrong: a high-support guideline that backfired, provenance silently dropped during consolidation, a swallowed API error that failed every task while producing a plausible log, and a baseline confound that nearly produced a misleading comparison with a prior method. We distill these into recommendations for teams deploying agent memory.

</details>


### [215] [World Models Favor Learned Priors over Visual Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26366)
*Han Xiao,Zhiyong Bao,Xiaotong Liu,Yanhao Chen,Qingqiang Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出视觉校准任务（用短视频中物体运动推断当前物理规则并迁移预测）与长尾偏置难题，构建TailWorld基准，并提出RIFT方法以解耦训练频率对规则推断的影响，提升罕见规则下的预测表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为每个环境单独训练世界模型不可扩展；跨环境预测需识别当前物理规则，而外观往往无法揭示规则。同时各物理条件数据量不均导致长尾训练分布，模型偏向常见规则，即使证据支持罕见规则。

Method: 构建TailWorld基准：评估已演示规则向新初始状态的迁移，并独立控制训练不均衡与校准信息量；通过同一预测初始状态的两条参考未来（演示规则 vs 常见规则）对比，量化预测向常见动力学的偏置。提出Rule Inference for Transfer（RIFT）：学习不同规则对物体运动的影响，从校准交互中推断当前规则，并应用于新初始状态的预测，显式控制训练频率对规则推断的影响。

Result: 在TailWorld上，RIFT在不同训练不均衡程度与分布偏移下提升了预测准确性与物理一致性，并在罕见动力学预测上取得增益。

Conclusion: 视觉校准中的长尾偏置是可度量、可缓解的问题；显式解耦训练频率与规则推断，使信息充分的观测证据能够支持罕见规则，是提升跨环境世界模型泛化的有效途径。

Abstract: World models are increasingly used to simulate physical interactions, but training a separate model for each environment limits scalability. Cross-environment prediction requires identifying the current physical rule, which appearance alone may not reveal. We therefore study visual calibration: using object motion and interactions in a short video as direct evidence to infer the current rule and predict motion under that rule from a different initial state. However, differences in data availability across physical conditions can yield a long-tailed training distribution, biasing models toward common rules even when evidence supports rare ones. To study this challenge, we introduce TailWorld, which evaluates the transfer of demonstrated rules across initial states while independently controlling training imbalance and calibration informativeness. The benchmark compares predictions with two reference futures from the same prediction initial state, one following the demonstrated rule and the other a common rule, to measure prediction bias toward common dynamics. We further propose Rule Inference for Transfer (RIFT), which learns how different rules affect object motion, infers the current rule from calibration interactions, and applies it to prediction from a new initial state. RIFT explicitly controls the influence of training frequency on rule inference, allowing sufficiently informative observational evidence to support rare rules. Experiments on TailWorld show that RIFT improves prediction accuracy and physical consistency across varying training imbalances and distribution shifts, with gains in predicting rare dynamics.

</details>


### [216] [ZeroVLN: Learning to Navigate without Supervised Fine-Tuning of the VLM Brain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26414)
*Zixuan Huang,Yang Zhou,Guli Zhang,Sunzhu Li,Hongyan Xie,Zehao Chen,Fuzhen Zhuang,Kaixuan Wang,Xiaozhi Chen,Yikun Ban,Deqing Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ZeroVLN 提出一种慢-快导航框架，通过语义工具调用接口让 VLM 大脑以自然语言下达移动决策，并由动作专家执行；在不进行导航域 SFT 的前提下，用闭环强化学习联合优化慢、快策略，在 VLN-CE 上显著优于代表性 SFT 层级方法，且几乎不损害 VLM 通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLN 方法通常将 VLM 大脑与可学习动作头耦合，并在导航域数据上微调 VLM 以预测几何目标；这种适配会大幅改变基础 VLM 权重，损害其通用视觉能力。作者希望在没有导航域监督微调的情况下实现有效导航，从而保留 VLM 的通用能力。

Method: 提出 ZeroVLN 慢-快框架：为 VLM 大脑配备语义工具调用接口，用自然语言向动作专家传达移动决策而非直接预测几何目标。训练分两阶段：先适配动作专家执行这些移动请求，再通过闭环强化学习（agentic RL）联合改进大脑的决策与动作专家的连续执行；整个过程不进行导航域 SFT。

Result: 在相同的导航情节上训练，ZeroVLN 在标准 VLN-CE 基准上显著优于代表性的基于 SFT 的层级 VLN 方法，同时对大脑权重的改动很小，基本保留了其通用能力。

Conclusion: ZeroVLN 是首个在 VLM 大脑上实现零导航域 SFT 的慢-快 VLN 框架，也是首个在 VLN-CE 中通过闭环 agentic RL 联合优化慢、快策略的工作；验证了语义接口+强化学习可替代导航域 SFT 的有效范式。

Abstract: Representative vision-language navigation (VLN) methods typically couple a vision-language model (VLM) brain with a learnable action head and fine-tune the VLM brain on navigation-domain data to predict geometric goals. However, such adaptation can substantially alter base VLM weights and degrade general visual capabilities. We introduce ZeroVLN, a slow-fast navigation framework that achieves effective navigation with zero navigation-domain SFT of the VLM brain. Rather than teaching the brain to predict geometric goals, we equip it with a semantic tool-calling interface for conveying movement decisions to an action expert in natural language. We first adapt the action expert to carry out these movement requests, then jointly improve the brain's movement decisions and the action expert's continuous execution through closed-loop reinforcement learning (RL). Trained on the same navigation episodes, ZeroVLN substantially outperforms representative SFT-based hierarchical VLN methods on standard VLN-CE (continuous environments) benchmarks, while inducing only minor changes to the brain's weights and largely preserving its general capabilities. To our knowledge, ZeroVLN is the first slow-fast VLN framework with zero navigation-domain SFT of the VLM brain, and the first to jointly optimize its slow and fast policies through closed-loop agentic RL in VLN-CE.

</details>


### [217] [Reasoning-Trace Analysis Using Large Language Models for Interpretable Q-Matrix Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26422)
*Jihong Zhang,Zijing Jin,Mingfeng Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出推理轨迹分析（RTA）框架，用思维链提示让大模型生成带属性标签的推理步骤，从而可重建、比较与审查Q矩阵中每个题目-属性指派；轨迹可提供矩阵层面一致性与拟合度无法提供的可检验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究仅评估大模型生成Q矩阵的最终结果，未考察单个题目-属性指派的依据，这种不透明性限制了AI在测量学中的负责任使用。

Method: RTA：通过CoT提示使LLM输出带属性标签的推理步骤，再分析这些步骤以重建、比较和检查每个指派。模拟研究采用扰动分数减法题目，四个LLM；实证研究含11道分数减法题、536名被试、四个LLM、至多三个温度、每条件100次重复，并对比CoT派生Q矩阵与直接生成Q矩阵及参考Q矩阵的重叠度和反应拟合。

Result: 模拟研究中四个LLM均能识别题目所需属性并排除不需要的属性。实证中CoT派生Q矩阵与参考Q矩阵平均重叠.809，低于直接生成的.877，且跨重复波动更大。轨迹审查将分歧归因于题型编码、标注规则及运算被表述为步骤的方式。重叠度相近的Q矩阵对作答反应的拟合不同。

Conclusion: RTA为Q矩阵生成提供可审查的证据，弥补矩阵级一致性与拟合指标之不足，应作为专家评审与实证验证的补充手段。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate Q-matrices for diagnostic classification models that approximate expert specifications, but evaluations have focused on the final matrices, leaving the basis of individual item-attribute assignments unexamined. This opacity limits the responsible use of AI in measurement. We propose Reasoning-Trace Analysis (RTA), a framework in which an LLM generates attribute-labeled reasoning steps through chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting and these steps are analyzed to reconstruct, compare, and inspect each assignment. RTA is intended to supplement expert review and empirical validation. In a simulation study with perturbed fraction subtraction items, four LLMs recovered the required attributes and withheld attributes the items did not require. In an empirical study with 11 fraction subtraction items, responses from 536 examinees, four LLMs, up to three temperatures, and 100 replications per condition, CoT-derived Q-matrices overlapped substantially with the reference Q-matrix (mean overlap .809) but less than directly generated Q-matrices (.877), and they varied more across replications. Inspection of the traces linked disagreements to item-type coding, labeling rules, and how operations were expressed as steps. Q-matrices with similar overlap differed in fit to examinee responses. RTA provides inspectable evidence that matrix-level agreement and fit do not.

</details>


### [218] [Seer: Guiding Small Deep-Research Agents with Shared Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26423)
*Yuhao Zhou,Yang Zhou,Yuanxi Li,Jindi Lv,Dan Si,Qing Ye,Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Seer 是一个推理时框架，将智能体的动作提议与学到的后果预测和价值估计解耦，用离线训练的模拟器和价值估计器为设备端小型研究智能体提供本地引导，显著提升多种模型的深度研究表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私与本地控制需求推动在用户设备上运行深度研究智能体，但设备端内存与算力有限，倾向使用小模型且难以在设备上训练。推理时引导面临三大障碍：真实执行来探索候选会增加工具调用；提示式自评估受限于智能体自身判断力；模型专属引导导致重复适配成本。

Method: 将动作提议与后果预测、价值估计分离。基于共享的真实研究轨迹语料训练两个模型：在观测到的调用—响应对上训练工具响应模拟器，在带折扣终止正确性标签的历史上训练价值估计器；二者离线训练后可跨智能体复用而无需再训练。执行阶段仅执行被选中的动作，只把真实工具响应写入已提交历史，避免对丢弃候选产生额外工具调用与推理开销。

Result: 在多个深度研究基准上大幅提升不同语言模型的智能体表现，例如 GAIA 准确率：Qwen3.5-4B 由 13.6% 提升至 41.7%，Qwen3.5-9B 由 22.3% 提升至 50.5%。分析表明更广的候选覆盖可补偿不完美模拟，而预测与打分误差会限制扩大想象搜索的收益。

Conclusion: 可复用的预测与评估模型能通过额外的本地计算增强小型研究智能体，且无需真实执行被丢弃的候选，体现了设备端推理时引导的可行路径。

Abstract: Deep-research agents repeatedly decide what to search, read, and verify, often using large cloud-hosted language models for action proposal. However, privacy and local control motivate running agents on users' devices, where limited memory and compute favor smaller models and complicate on-device training. Yet inference-time guidance faces three obstacles: exploring alternatives through real execution adds tool calls, prompted self-evaluation is bounded by the agent's judgment, and model-specific guidance risks repeated adaptation costs. We introduce Seer, an inference-time framework providing local guidance that separates the agent's action proposal from learned consequence prediction and value estimation. From a shared corpus of real research trajectories, we train two models: a tool-response simulator on observed call--response pairs and a value estimator on histories labeled with discounted terminal correctness. The simulator and estimator are trained offline and reused across agents without further training. Moreover, Seer executes only the selected action and adds only real tool responses to the committed history, avoiding tool calls on discarded candidates at additional inference cost. Across multiple deep-research benchmarks, Seer substantially improves agent performance for different language models. For instance, GAIA accuracy rises from 13.6% to 41.7% for Qwen3.5-4B and from 22.3% to 50.5% for Qwen3.5-9B. Furthermore, our analyses suggest that broader candidate coverage can compensate for imperfect simulation, while prediction and scoring errors limit the benefits of wider imagined search. These findings highlight the potential of reusable models for prediction and evaluation to strengthen small research agents through extra local computation without the real execution of the discarded candidates.

</details>


### [219] [A CPU Inside an LLM: Zero-Gradient Compilation of Exact Circuits into Pretrained Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26450)
*Tanmay Sah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出零梯度、闭式的“电路嫁接”原语，将确定性多位数算术直接编译进预训练语言模型权重，无需训练样本与运行时钩子，在保持自然语言能力的同时实现近100%精确算术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练语言模型在多位数精确算术上仍不可靠，通常依赖梯度微调或外部工具解释器，二者分别带来训练成本、能力退化与部署开销，因此需要一种无需训练、无运行时开销即可注入精确计算的途径。

Method: 三项解析机制：(1) 在无偏置SwiGLU/GeGLU上做两层Mellin-GLU求积，将未知的逐token RMSNorm缩放转化为对数间隔门控网格上的平移（动态范围>250×）并给出IEEE 754误差界；(2) 通过带符号Chebyshev不定RoPE自汇注意力与无记忆SiLU反对称GatedDeltaNet移位实现跨token尺度抵消；(3) 轴对齐宽度扩展(d→d+K)配合√(d/(d+K))范数补偿、Base-1000规范BPE输出与差分(+254,−256)绑定去嵌入；并由自动5阶段编译器综合出常数TC⁰深度的加/减/乘电路、布尔电路及5比特多数/奇偶电路。

Result: 6位数算术(N=1,200, BF16)：Granite 4.2 3B(活跃非零1.07M–1.33M, ≤0.036%)与Qwen 3.5 4B(2.25M–2.39M, ≤0.057%)分别达99.17%与100.00% ALU前缀精度(FP32 sidecar下100%)，句中恢复率98%–100%，显著优于12.8K样本LoRA(42.2%/61.7%, 0%行内续写)；布尔电路100%(N=1,728)，多数/奇偶100%；分块稀疏sidecar在A100上显存7.89/10.65 GiB(INT8/INT4+FP32 sidecar为4.60/8.14 GiB)，并通过全部14/14预注册长文本Tango切分(0/11,134 OOD触发)。

Conclusion: 该工作表明精确算术可作为闭式结构编译进冻结权重，在不训练、不挂钩子的条件下兼顾精度、自然语言能力与部署可行性，为模型能力注入提供了区别于微调与工具调用的第三条路径。

Abstract: Pretrained language models (LMs) remain unreliable on exact multi-digit arithmetic without gradient fine-tuning or external tool interpreters. We introduce closed-form, zero-gradient circuit-grafting primitives that compile deterministic computation directly into pretrained LM weights using zero training examples and zero runtime hooks, while bounding per-layer normalization drift and preserving natural-language (NL) competence. Our construction combines three analytical mechanisms: (1) two-layer Mellin-GLU quadrature over bias-free SwiGLU/GeGLU layers, turning unknown per-token RMSNorm scaling into translation along a log-spaced gate grid ($>250\times$ dynamic range) with IEEE 754 error bounds; (2) cross-token scale cancellation via signed Chebyshev Indefinite-RoPE self-sink attention and memoryless SiLU-antisymmetric GatedDeltaNet shifts; and (3) axis-aligned width extension ($d \to d+K$) with $\sqrt{d/(d+K)}$ norm compensation, Base-1000 canonical BPE emission, and differential $(+254,-256)$ tied unembeddings. An automated 5-stage compiler synthesizes 4-8 digit signed $\{+,-,\times\}$ circuits in constant $\mathsf{TC}^0$ depth alongside 3-literal Boolean (100%, $N=1{,}728$; plus 42-layer Gemma 4 E4B audits) and 5-bit Majority/Parity (100%) circuits. On 6-digit arithmetic ($N=1{,}200$, BF16), IBM Granite 4.2 3B (1.07M-1.33M active non-zeros, $\le 0.036\%$) and Alibaba Qwen 3.5 4B (2.25M-2.39M non-zeros, $\le 0.057\%$) reach 99.17% and 100.00% ALU-prefix accuracy (100% with an FP32 sidecar) and 98%-100% mid-sentence resumption, outperforming 12.8K-sample LoRA (42.2% / 61.7%, 0% inline continuation). A factorized block-sparse sidecar holds A100 memory at 7.89 / 10.65 GiB (4.60 / 8.14 GiB under INT8/INT4 + FP32 sidecar) while passing all 14/14 pre-registered $>2$K-token Tango splits ($N=4{,}392$; 0/11,134 OOD triggers).

</details>


### [220] [Monte Carlo Pass Search for Setpieces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26464)
*Andrew Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出蒙特卡洛传球搜索（MCPS），将定位球视为「执行方案与未来演化的概率分布」而非单次已发生事件，用世界模型+价值模型对反事实传中与站位进行搜索和估值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定位球贡献约1/3的非点球xG，但传统评估只看「接下来发生了什么」，属于结果导向的事后评价，无法回答「本可以怎样踢得更好」。作者希望建立一种反事实、分布式的定位球评估与搜索框架。

Method: MCPS：从开球前状态出发，(1) 生成反事实传球方案（角球/高球任意球）与反事实站位（进攻球员开球时位置）；(2) 在世界模型中多次rollout——3D球体物理、22名球员的生成模型、门将出击/留守决策、Player-to-Touch模型；(3) 用价值模型为每个未来打分。数据为30场意甲2025/26赛季的249次传中，所有模型仅在未见过的比赛上评估（严格样本外）。

Result: 最优可选传球比实际执行的传球多0.0085 xG（+28%），主要来自「先于门将触达进攻球员」；仅搜索站位在观测球上+0.0027 xG，在最优球基础上再+0.0033 xG；进一步设计跑动路线再+0.0007 xG。文中还给出保持搜索可信的方法，并估计各组件所需数据量。

Conclusion: 定位球质量可通过反事实搜索显著提升，传球选择是主要增益来源，站位与跑动为次级增益；但绝对增益较小，且离俱乐部可依赖的水平仍需更多数据，需重视搜索流程的诚实性（防泄漏、样本外评估）。

Abstract: Set pieces produce about a third of non-penalty expected goals (xG), yet they are usually evaluated by what happened next. We present Monte Carlo Pass Search (MCPS) for set pieces, which treats a corner or a lofted free kick as a distribution over plausible executions and plausible futures instead of a single realised event. From the pre-kick state, MCPS proposes counterfactual deliveries and counterfactual set-ups (where the attackers stand at the kick), plays each out many times in a world model (3D ball physics, a generative model of all 22 players, the keeper's come-or-stay decision and a Player-to-Touch model) and scores every future with a value model. On 249 deliveries from 30 Serie A 2025/26 matches, with every model applied only to matches it never saw, the best available delivery is worth 0.0085 xG more than the one kicked (+28 %), mainly because it reaches an attacker before the keeper. Searching where the attackers stand as well adds +0.0027 xG with the observed ball and +0.0033 on top of the best ball; designing their runs too adds +0.0007 more. We show how to keep such a search honest, and estimate how much more data each component needs before a club could rely on it.

</details>


### [221] [FLUID: Flexible, Evidence-Grounded Situated Task Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26491)
*Yunqi Zhao,Run Peng,Ze Dong,Ryan Lu,Yinpei Dai,Joyce Chai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FLUID框架，用真实人际指导数据对多模态任务指导中的"说什么、何时说、说不说"进行证据化建模与评估，并构建实时指导智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将合成对话当作固定目标，忽视了实时任务指导的灵活性：多种回复与时序均可能合理，有时最佳动作是保持沉默。

Method: 基于WTaG与HoloAssist的自然人-人指导数据，将干预行为锚定到其支撑证据、必要性与时序灵活性表示上；该表示同时用于评估灵活指导并构建实时指导智能体。

Result: 该智能体在响应正确性、干预精确率、及时性与响应延迟的综合平衡上优于所评估系统，但在避免语音重叠方面仍有改进空间。

Conclusion: FLUID将情境化任务指导从刚性参考匹配推进到更自然、自适应的自然人-AI交互。

Abstract: Real-time task guidance is inherently flexible: when an agent guides a user through a task, multiple responses and timings can be appropriate, and sometimes the best action is to remain silent. Existing benchmarks often miss this flexibility by treating synthesized dialogues as fixed targets for what to say and when to say it. We introduce FLUID (Flexible, Evidence-Grounded Situated Task Guidance), a unified framework for modeling, evaluating, and enabling flexible interaction in multimodal task guidance. Using naturally occurring human-human guidance from WTaG and HoloAssist, FLUID grounds interventions in their supporting evidence, necessity, and timing flexibility, and uses this representation both to evaluate flexible guidance and to build a real-time agent for task guidance. Our agent achieves the strongest overall balance of response correctness, intervention precision, timely guidance, and response latency among evaluated systems, while still leaving room for improvement in avoiding speech overlap. Together, FLUID moves situated task guidance beyond rigid reference matching toward more natural and adaptive human-AI interaction. Project page: https://fluid-guidance.github.io.

</details>


### [222] [GLiDE: A Fast, Calibrated Decision Model Distilled from Reasoning Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26509)
*Sahibzada Allahyar,Julia White,Dhruv Atreja,George Hurn-Maloney,Urchade Zaratiana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GLiDE 系列决策模型（0.4B–27B）通过去掉全注意力层的因果掩码实现双向交互，并用多教师软标签蒸馏与渐进式后训练，将推理能力迁移到单次前向推理，在性能-参数 Pareto 前沿上领先同级模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策模型单次前向打分即可给出置信度感知决策，但推理能显著提升决策质量；核心问题是能否在不生成文本/中间推理的前提下保留推理带来的收益。

Method: 基于 Qwen 混合语言模型，移除全注意力层因果掩码，使上下文、问题与候选答案双向交互；采用含推理模型在内的多教师软标签蒸馏，配合多样决策任务的渐进式后训练；另探索无参数循环深度以在不生成 token 的情况下增加推理期计算。

Result: 五个规模均位于 Hugging Face DI 的性能-参数 Pareto 前沿，优于同尺寸或更小的对比模型；GLiDE 27B 隐藏集得分 71.52，ECE 2.0%，单 GPU 中位延迟 92.9 ms；经检查点平均与无参数循环深度后达 73.37，ECE 2.1%。

Conclusion: 推理收益可以在不显式生成推理内容的前提下被蒸馏进单次前向决策模型，且通过检查点平均与递归深度进一步提升质量，兼具高精度、良好校准与低延迟。

Abstract: Decision models enable fast, confidence-aware decisions by scoring candidate answers in a single forward pass, without generating text or intermediate reasoning. Yet reasoning can substantially improve decision quality, raising the question of whether its benefits can be retained without the cost of generating it. We investigate this question with \textbf{GLiDE}, a family of decision models spanning 0.4B to 27B parameters.
  Building on Qwen hybrid language models, GLiDE enables bidirectional interactions between the context, question, and candidate answers by removing causal masking from full-attention layers. To transfer reasoning capabilities into single-pass inference, we combine soft-target distillation from multiple teachers, including a reasoning model, with progressive post-training on diverse decision tasks. We further explore parameter-free recurrent depth to increase inference-time computation without generating tokens.
  Across five model sizes, GLiDE lies on the performance--parameter Pareto frontier of the Hugging Face \DI{}, with each model outperforming evaluated alternatives of equal or smaller size. \dmtwo{} (27B) achieves a hidden-set score of 71.52, with an expected calibration error of 2.0\% and a median latency of 92.9\,ms on a single GPU. Further checkpoint averaging and parameter-free recurrent depth yield GLiDE~27B, which reaches 73.37 with an expected calibration error of 2.1\%.

</details>


### [223] [LLM GerryMandering: Separating Interactions from Additive Effects in LLM Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26538)
*George Rahul,Ronit Jahilal,Bhaskara Hanuma Vedula,Ponnurangam Kumaraguru,Pushkar Shukla*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有交叉性偏见审计缺乏可靠基线，"超过任一单属性即为交叉"的标准判据会在无交互数据上大量误报；作者提出ADDIS（加性残差距离+置换零分布检验），将假阳性从66.1降至0.09个/数据集，并能在13个开源模型上把判据标记率从7.1%降至3.0%，保留少量真实交互核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交叉性偏见（模型对同时持有多个边缘化身份者的处理偏离各属性独立效应的预测）审计困难：交叉子组数量二次增长，且"独立属性本应预测什么"缺乏公认基线。多数审计以"超过任一单属性即标记"为标准，但属性独立作用时该条件本身已可满足，导致判据缺乏特异性。

Method: 1) 构建150个无交互的合成数据集，检验"超过任一单属性"标准与shuffle零分布的表现；2) 植入已知大小的交互，检验修复后零分布的召回；3) 提出ADDIS：对所有属性拟合加性模型，以子组的加性残差距离（ARD）为统计量，用Freedman-Lane置换零分布做检验；4) 构建IndiPersona（10,000个印度合成人物，6个身份属性），审计13个开源语言模型，并用反事实人物做验证。

Result: 合成基准上，标准判据+shuffle零分布平均每数据集误报66.1个子组（每个数据集至少1个）；修复零分布后仅能召回32%的植入交互。ADDIS误报0.09个/数据集，召回84%。真实模型审计中，738个测试子组里标准判据平均标记7.1%，ADDIS标记3.0%；性别认同×性取向在所有模型中均为最大配对；反事实人物确认该结果，并揭示几乎所有模型中存在观测检验因数据不足而无法检出的种姓×阶层交互。

Conclusion: 审计报告的大量"交叉性偏见"可能仅源于独立的单属性效应，但存在一小部分真实交互稳健存留；ADDIS提供了兼具低假阳性与高召回的可操作基线方案。

Abstract: Intersectional bias arises when a model treats people who hold several marginalised identities at once differently from what their separate identities would predict. Auditing such intersectional subgroups remains a challenge: their number grows quadratically, and there is no agreed baseline for what independent attributes would predict. Without such a baseline, many existing audits flag a subgroup when it exceeds each attribute alone, a condition that attributes acting independently already satisfy. We test this standard criterion on 150 synthetic datasets built with no interaction. With the shuffle null, it flags 66.1 false subgroups per dataset on average, and at least one in every dataset. When we plant interactions of known size into these datasets, a repaired null lets the criterion recover only 32% of them. We introduce ADDIS (Additive Intersectional Screen), which fits an additive model over all attributes, scores each subgroup by its Additive Residual Distance (ARD) from that model's prediction, and tests it against a Freedman-Lane null. On our synthetic benchmark, ADDIS flags 0.09 false subgroups per dataset and recovers 84% of the same planted interactions. We then build IndiPersona, 10,000 synthetic Indian personas with six identity attributes, and audit 13 open language models. The standard criterion flags 7.1% of the 738 tested subgroups on average; ADDIS flags 3.0%. Gender identity crossed with sexual orientation is the largest pairing in every model, and counterfactual personas confirm it and additionally reveal a caste x class interaction in all but one model that the observational test lacked the data to detect. Much of what audits report as intersectional bias can thus arise from independent single-attribute effects alone, yet a small core of interactions survives.

</details>


### [224] [The Geometry of Lies: Knowledge and Persona Directions for Detecting Deception in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26545)
*Md. Abdullah Al Zami,Suraiya Shanaz,Mahdi Hasan,Shoaib Ahmed Dipu,Farig Yousuf Sadeque,Md. Sabbir Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文系统检验了基于"人设方向"（honest vs. deceptive role-play的免标注方向）的线性探针在70B规模及"知识型谎言"上的泛化能力，发现其跨数据集优势有限、主要受措辞驱动，但与知识方向相加后可超过同深度监督探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性探针是成本最低的LLM测谎手段，但难以跨谎言类型迁移；免标注的诚实/欺骗角色扮演方向此前未在70B规模验证，也未在"知识型谎言"上检验——这类场景中"撒谎"与"诚实否认"字面可能完全相同，构成对方向可分性的严峻考验。

Method: 扩展已发表的persona bank，加入"否认自己掌握的知识"的角色及对应诚实角色；取各模型中间层的persona direction，与单数据集探针、随机persona bank方向作对照；构建knowledge direction（对比"模型可答的问题"与"无人可答的问题"，后接同一脚本化否认）；在Liars' Bench、Gender-Secret微调等设定上评估，并以随机方向带（random-direction band）作为显著性参照。

Result: persona direction在4个模型中的3个上对Liars' Bench谎言排序优于单数据集探针，Qwen上失败（尽管其保持角色率更高）；Llama-3.3-70B上优于随机bank，但相对已发表方向的增益落在随机扩展范围内。知识型谎言上，persona方向主要追踪措辞：在说谎与诚实否认措辞相同时，Llama/Qwen的三个主要对比落在随机带内，Gemma仅达边缘，Mistral有一项突破。knowledge direction在2个模型上、第3个模型上勉强区分相同否认，第4个失败，且部分结果依赖基准措辞。Gender-Secret上Llama超出随机带数个百分点，Mistral失败。两方向相加在4个模型上均优于同深度监督探针（其中一个两者近随机），但在Llama上仅与persona方向均值持平，且在留出谎言上落后于该探针。更丰富的角色扮演问题不能提升Liars' Bench上的表现。

Conclusion: 免标注persona/知识方向在70B上具有一定跨谎言类型排序能力，但增益微弱且多落在随机方向上界之内，知识型谎言上的判别很大程度上依赖措辞而非内在欺骗状态；组合方向可望替代监督探针，但泛化到新谎言类型仍不可靠，需更强去措辞混淆的设计。

Abstract: Linear probes are the cheapest LLM lie detectors but often fail to transfer across kinds of lie. Label-free directions from honest and deceptive role-play are untested at 70B scale and on lies about knowledge, where a lie and an honest denial can be identical. We widen a published persona bank with roles that deny knowledge they have, plus honest counterparts. At each model's middle layer the resulting persona direction ranks lies across Liars' Bench better than single-dataset probes on three of its four models; it fails on Qwen, even when Qwen stays in role far more often. On Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct it beats directions from a random persona bank, but its gain over the published direction is within the range of random widenings. On lies about knowledge, persona directions mainly track a reply's wording: where lies and honest denials share their words, our three main contrasts stay inside the random-direction band on Llama and Qwen and reach only its edge on Gemma, though on Mistral one clears it. A knowledge direction contrasts questions the model can answer with ones nobody can, each followed by the same scripted denial. It separates lies from identical honest denials on two models and narrowly on a third, but not on the fourth; its other results partly depend on the benchmark's wording. On the Gender-Secret fine-tune it clears the band by a few hundredths on Llama and fails on Mistral. The sum of the two directions ranks lies better than a supervised probe at the same depth on all four models (on one, both near chance) but is level with the persona direction on Llama's average and behind that probe on held-out lies. More varied role-play questions do not help the persona direction on Liars' Bench.

</details>


### [225] [MM-SystemOneBias: Evaluating Bias in Multimodal Jev-Style System One Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26557)
*Jinzhe Li,Qi Wang,Dandan Guo,Li Liu,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MM-SystemOneBias基准，用6种配对干预在9个数据集、11类任务上比较13个多模态决策模型与其骨干模型，评估语义决策在呈现变化下的稳定性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将决策界面偏差与多模态模型行为分开考察，缺少统一协议来在实际任务上比较多模态决策模型与其骨干模型

Method: 构建MM-SystemOneBias：13个模型、9个公开数据集共5,323个原始实例、11类任务场景，六种配对干预——选项顺序、选项标签、外部判断、图文描述、无关信息、评分量表；并分析7组匹配的决策模型—骨干对

Result: 7对中决策模型在选项标签、无关信息、评分量表变化下全部更稳定；6对选项顺序翻转率更低；7对在错误外部判断下准确率损失更小，6对在冲突描述下损失更小；6对从正确外部判断中获益更少、在一致描述下准确率提升更少；语义决策未变时，答案概率平均绝对变化达4.56–11.55个百分点

Conclusion: 决策模型对呈现层干扰更稳健，但对任务一致证据的利用率也更低；仅看准确率会掩盖显著的输入效应，需同时考察概率层面的偏移

Abstract: When multimodal decision models determine actions, moderation outcomes, or quality judgments, their reliability depends on preserving semantic decisions across presentation changes, ignoring irrelevant additions, and distinguishing information consistent with task evidence from information that conflicts with it. Existing evaluations study decision-interface biases and multimodal model behavior in separate settings, leaving no common protocol to compare multimodal decision models with their backbones on practical tasks. We introduce \textbf{MM-SystemOneBias}, which evaluates 13 models on 5,323 original instances from nine public datasets and 11 task scenarios using six paired interventions: option order, option label, external judgment, image--text description, irrelevant information, and rating scale. Across seven matched decision-model--backbone pairs, decision models are more stable under option-label, irrelevant-information, and rating-scale changes in all seven pairs; six pairs also show lower option-order flip rates. They incur smaller accuracy losses under incorrect external judgments in all seven pairs and conflicting descriptions in six. In six pairs, they gain less accuracy from correct external judgments and show less favorable accuracy changes under consistent descriptions. Among pairs with unchanged semantic decisions, mean absolute changes in answer probabilities range from 4.56 to 11.55 percentage points, revealing input effects that accuracy alone misses.

</details>


### [226] [ProtoDiffusion: Token-per-Layer Generative Modeling of Multi-Layer Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26586)
*Adrià Ciurana Lanau,Harleen Hanspal,Lusine Abrahamyan,Emilio Tylson Baixauli,Yu Cao,Danai Triantafyllidou,Stefano Zorzi,Gustavo Zanoni Felipe,Peter Griger,Stefan Sietzen,Petru-Daniel Tudosiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProtoDiffusion 基于 ProtoVAE，将多层设计编码为一个共享空间原型加每层一个候选向量（N 层仅需 HW+N 个 token，而非 NHW），并在此紧凑潜空间上联合去噪原型与候选槽，统一支持图像/文本到分层设计的生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层生成模型为每层分配整画布或区域大小的空间潜变量网格，表示规模随图层累计空间范围线性增长（NHW），且通常需要预先给定层数与布局。

Method: 构造 ProtoVAE：多层 VAE，将设计编码为一个共享空间原型和每层一个候选向量，候选向量条件化共享解码器以重建对应 RGBA 层；在潜空间上构建 ProtoDiffusion，联合去噪原型与固定数量的候选槽，以图像或文本为条件、边界框为可选布局引导；模型可将多余槽置空并自行决定层数，无框时自行推断布局；同一架构兼容 image-to-design 与 text-to-design。

Result: ProtoVAE 在层裁剪上达 40.43 dB PSNR；LayerD 评测集上 image-to-design 的 RGB L1 为有框 0.040、无框 0.057，有框 alpha soft IoU 0.850，接近最强微调基线 0.871；合并图像上 text-to-design 的 FID 为 66.41（FLUX.2 [klein] 为 86.84），文本准确率 0.860，比同骨干的“先生成后分解”级联快 19.1 倍。

Conclusion: 以共享原型替代逐层网格的紧凑分层潜表示可在保持高保真重建的同时显著压缩表示规模，并使层数、布局推断与图像/文本双模态生成统一在单一扩散框架内，兼顾质量与效率。

Abstract: Graphic designs are ordered compositions of RGBA layers whose separation enables selection, movement, and reuse. Existing layered models represent each layer with a full-canvas or region-sized spatial latent grid, so representation size grows with the cumulative spatial extent of the layers. We present ProtoDiffusion, a latent-diffusion framework built on ProtoVAE, a multi-layer variational autoencoder that encodes a design into one shared spatial prototype and one candidate vector per layer. Each candidate conditions a shared decoder to reconstruct its corresponding RGBA layer. For $N$ layers, the representation contains $HW+N$ tokens before backbone-specific packing, compared with $NHW$ for full-grid-per-layer representations. ProtoDiffusion jointly denoises the prototype and a fixed set of candidate slots, conditioned on an image or text, with bounding boxes as optional layout guidance. The model leaves unneeded slots empty and determines the number of layers and, in absence of boxes, their layout, requiring neither as input. The same architecture supports both image-to-design and text-to-design. ProtoVAE achieves high-fidelity reconstruction from its compact shared latent representation, reaching 40.43 dB PSNR on layer crops. On the LayerD evaluation split, image-to-design achieves RGB L1 scores of 0.040 with bounding boxes and 0.057 without, with box-guided alpha soft IoU (0.850) comparable to the strongest fine-tuned baseline (0.871). On merged images, text-to-design reaches 66.41 FID versus 86.84 for FLUX.2 [klein], achieves a 0.860 text-accuracy score, and runs $19.1\times$ faster than the same-backbone generation-then-decomposition cascade.

</details>


### [227] [Probing the "Conscious" Mind: Selective Access and Global Workspace in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26590)
*Jiahao Huang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出多模态概念透镜（MC-Lens），一种基于Jacobian的多模态透镜，将视觉编码器与语言解码器的中间状态映射到由解码器输出词表定义的共享语义空间，用以检验全局工作台理论（GWT）在VLM中的可实现性，并支持内部概念干预与跨模态概念对齐分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM整合视觉与文本信息，但其输出并不揭示内部概念表征；同时GWT关于"信息从并行过程中被选择、在共享工作台整合并供专门过程调用"的假设缺乏多模态人工系统中的实验检验手段。作者希望建立可读、可干预的概念层面分析工具。

Method: 提出MC-Lens：由视觉编码器透镜与语言解码器透镜构成的Jacobian透镜，将中间状态映射到以解码器输出词表为锚的共享语义空间，从而跨模态比较概念可及性；支持对内部概念进行干预（反事实实验）；将"readout carrier"从单一名称token扩展为token集合以更完整地刻画概念信息；并设计两个J-space指标追踪跨模态概念对齐与选择性访问的发展。

Result: (i) 多模态概念信息在中间状态中可读出，并随任务需求被选择性访问；(ii) 反事实实验表明被访问的概念信息参与下游计算与推理；(iii) 两个J-space指标在受控VLM训练中比隐状态余弦相似度更清晰地刻画跨模态概念对齐的发展，且可靠的选择性访问晚于概念可及性出现；(iv) 跨不同VLM系列与规模，尽管内部组织存在差异，概念可及性、查询依赖的选择性与跨任务干预效应呈现共享模式。

Conclusion: 该工作为在多模态人工系统中研究GWT提供了实验基础，并表明所揭示的共享功能模式可为改进跨模态对齐提供指导。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) integrate visual and textual information, but their outputs do not reveal internal concept representations. Global workspace theory (GWT) proposes that information is selected from parallel brain processes, integrated in a shared workspace, and made available to specialised processes. We introduce the Multimodal Concept Lens (MC-Lens), a multimodal Jacobian lens, to study these functions in VLM concept processing. Its vision encoder and language decoder lenses map intermediate states to a shared semantic space defined by the decoder's output vocabulary to compare concept accessibility across modalities. MC-Lens supports internal concept interventions to examine the role of accessed concepts in downstream computation. (i) Accessibility and selective access. Multimodal concept information is readable in intermediate states and selectively accessible as tasks require. We extend "readout carriers" from one name token to a token set to better capture and express concept information and support counterfactual experiments. (ii) Concepts in downstream computation. Counterfactual experiments show that accessed concept information participates in downstream computation and reasoning. (iii) Development of cross-modal alignment and selective access. We develop two J-space metrics tracking the development of cross-modal concept alignment more clearly than hidden state cosine in controlled VLM training. The experiment also shows that reliable selective access develops later than concept accessibility. (iv) Shared functions across models. Across VLM families and sizes, we find shared patterns of concept accessibility, query-dependent selectivity and cross-task intervention effects despite differences in internal organisation. These findings offer an experimental basis to study GWT in multimodal artificial systems and how it can guide cross-modal alignment improvements.

</details>


### [228] [PlayCraft: Designing Engaging Games from Agent Play Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26624)
*Weihao Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PlayCraft:一个用视觉智能体反复试玩并据其试玩报告自动改写游戏、以提升"精通度(mastery)"的自动化游戏设计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI已能生成可玩的游戏,但这些游戏未必具有吸引力;而"精通"(玩家技能成长)是参与度的关键来源,传统上 mastery 的设计依赖大量人类试玩与人工调参,成本高且难以自动化。

Method: 采用"游玩—制作"交替循环:(1)游玩阶段:视觉玩家智能体观察游戏画面、操作控件、在多次 episode 中追求目标并保留记忆,每局结束后汇报进度、结果、决策与反思;(2)制作阶段:制作智能体基于报告估计玩家能力、预测不同熟练度的表现,与目标学习曲线对比,诊断差距并修改游戏,使玩家通过更优决策与执行获得有意义的提升机会。评估方式为在新一轮运行中用三种智能水平设置的玩家智能体测学习增益(learning gain)与学习斜率(learning slope)。

Result: 与初始游戏相比,PlayCraft 在多数游戏中带来更大的学习增益与更陡的学习斜率;在每一种智能水平设置下,两项指标的宏平均均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于智能体试玩经验、以目标学习曲线为参照的自动游戏修订能有效增强游戏的 mastery 设计,为自动化且具吸引力的游戏生成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent AI agents can generate playable games, but these games are not necessarily engaging. Mastery is a key source of engagement, yet designing for it requires extensive human play and tuning. We propose PlayCraft, an automated framework that uses a vision-based agent to play games and draws on its detailed play experience to revise them for mastery. Starting from an initial game, PlayCraft alternates between playing and crafting. In the playing stage, a player agent watches the game screen, uses the controls, and pursues the game objective over repeated episodes while retaining memory. After each episode, it reports its progress, outcomes, decisions, and reflections. In the crafting stage, a crafter agent uses these reports to revise the game. It estimates player abilities, predicts performance at different proficiency levels, and compares those predictions with a target learning progression. It diagnoses the game and addresses the gaps by giving players meaningful opportunities to improve through better decisions and execution. We evaluate games in fresh runs with player agents across three intelligence settings, measuring learning gain and learning slope. Compared with the initial games, PlayCraft yields larger learning gains and steeper learning slopes in most games. It also outperforms the baselines on the macro-average of each metric at every intelligence setting.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [229] [Asymmetric rhetorical strategies drive partisanship in television news](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25140)
*Upasana Dutta,Homa Hosseinmardi,Amir Ghasemian,Aaron Clauset,Duncan J. Watts*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究用大语言模型分析2013–2025年美国六大电视台逾40万条新闻节目，构建多维党派偏见度量，识别出8个可解释语言特征（解释73%党派方差），揭示各台在倾向性及修辞策略上的系统性不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电视仍是美国主要政治信息来源，但关于电视新闻中党派性如何表达的系统性证据有限；既有研究把党派性视为一维构念，难以揭示意识形态偏见背后的修辞策略。

Method: 使用大语言模型分析美国六大新闻台2013–2025年间超40万条新闻节目，构建多维党派偏见指标，提取8个可解释的语言特征；结果经训练有素的人类评分者验证，并用四个政治倾向不同的额外LLM进行复现。

Result: 8个语言特征解释73%的党派方差；MSNBC与CNN在共和党总统任期内左移、民主党任期内趋中，而Fox News在每次大选后无论结果如何都强化保守立场；MSNBC与CNN更多强调攻击共和党行为体而非强化民主党立场，Fox News则强化共和党观点远多于攻击民主党的观点；内群体赞扬与外群体攻击的关联在Fox News上比MSNBC强三倍。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了电视台层面意识形态倾向在修辞策略传达上的系统性差异，为媒体效果、受众态度与政治传播的后续研究提供新方向。

Abstract: Television remains a dominant source of political information in the United States, yet systematic evidence on how partisanship is expressed in televised news remains limited. Prior research has largely treated partisanship as a one-dimensional construct, offering limited insight into the rhetorical strategies that underlie ideological bias. Here, by analyzing over 400,000 news episodes using large language models (LLMs), we provide a multi-dimensional measure of partisanship bias in television news across six major U.S. news stations (2013--2025). We identify eight interpretable linguistic features that explain 73% of partisanship variance in television news.\ Our findings reveal systematic asymmetries across stations. While MSNBC and CNN shifted leftward during Republican presidencies and moderated under Democratic presidencies, Fox News increased its conservative alignment following each election regardless of the electoral outcome. Beyond aggregate trends, MSNBC and CNN emphasized attacks on Republican actors more than reinforcement of Democratic positions, whereas Fox News reinforced Republican viewpoints substantially more than it attacked Democratic viewpoints. The association between in-group praise and out-group attacks was also threefold stronger on Fox News than on MSNBC. These results are validated against trained human raters and replicated with four additional LLMs with differing political leanings. Together, our study reveals systematic station-level differences in the rhetorical strategies with which ideological slant is communicated in television news, opening new opportunities for future research on media effects, audience attitudes, and political communication.

</details>


### [230] [Crisis Governance at the Joints. Mapping the Ethical and Societal Territory of Crisis Management Technologies: The Case of Human-Swarm Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25202)
*Anthony Longo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出“3i框架”（Interface接口、Infrastructure基础设施、Imaginary想象域），用于系统梳理人-蜂群协同（HST）在危机治理中的伦理与社会议题，并强调责任鸿沟、有意义人类控制侵蚀等问题产生于三个层次之间的"接缝"，通过四种"转译"（习惯硬化为常规、常规变为叙事、原则变为程序、程序变为提示词）发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 危机与灾害治理日益依赖感知、分类与行动速度超越人类的技术；无人机蜂群与人协同（HST）既能提升态势感知，也引发人类控制、责任、监控、伤员分诊、军民两用与公共合法性等争议。现有讨论往往逐层处理，难以应对跨层次的紧迫问题，故需一个整合性的伦理-社会地图。

Method: 采用批判性文献综述，横跨技术哲学、政治理论、道德心理学、心灵哲学、AI伦理以及批判性灾害研究与安全研究，构建3i分析框架；以两个实例（worked examples）加以阐释，并据此推导设计与治理启示及面向开发者、应急组织与政策制定者的问题清单。

Result: 框架有效刻画了HST伦理社会议题的层次分布与跨层耦合机制：核心问题（责任鸿沟、有意义人类控制之侵蚀）并非单层失效，而是经由四种转译在层次接缝处生成；实例展示表明该框架可操作地定位风险节点，并由此导出面向设计、组织与政策的启示与问题集。

Conclusion: HST的伦理与社会治理不能逐层孤立处理，须以跨层视角关注"接缝"与转译过程；据此提出设计、治理层面的建议，并为开发者、应急组织与政策制定者提供一套反思性问题，以维护人类控制、责任可追溯与公共合法性。

Abstract: Crisis and disaster governance increasingly relies on technologies that sense, classify and act faster than humans can. Drone swarms coordinated with human operators, known as human-swarm teaming, provide a productive case to review the field of crisis governance. They promise better situational awareness in disaster response, but they also raise questions about human control, responsibility, surveillance, triage, dual use and public legitimacy. This report maps the ethical and societal territory that HST opens up. It unpacks this territory with a 3i-framework, the Interface, where operators and swarms work together, the Infrastructure, where organisations distribute tasks, decisions and blame, and the Imaginary, where law, policy and shared societal assumptions define what a crisis is and which interventions it justifies. The central claim is that these concerns cannot be addressed one level at a time. Many of the most pressing problems, such as responsibility gaps and the erosion of meaningful human control, arise at the joints between levels, through four translations, habits harden, routines become stories, principles become procedures, and procedures become prompts. Drawing on a critical review of literature across philosophy of technology, political theory, moral psychology, philosophy of mind, AI ethics, and critical disaster and security studies, the report develops this framework, illustrates it with two worked examples, and derives design and governance implications and a set of questions for developers, emergency organisations and policymakers.

</details>


### [231] [Whose Safety? Addressing Anti-Blackness in the Age of AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25302)
*Mírian Silva,Serena Oduro*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过分析2020–2025年十大会议69,290篇论文发现：AI安全研究占比从0.5%升至6.4%并超越AI伦理，但94%依赖自动指标、仅11%提及社会危害，2,182篇安全论文中仅8篇涉及种族且无一篇以黑人群体为中心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "AI安全"已取代"AI伦理"成为AI评估的主导框架，并在政治与资金影响下将公共政策焦点从当下切实危害转向"生存性风险"叙事。作者认为若不直面反黑人主义（anti-Blackness），安全本身无法实现，故需实证刻画安全研究对反黑人问题的处理状况。

Method: 以Sylvia Wynter关于"人"（Man）被"过度表征"的批判为理论框架——现代西方人性概念建立在对黑人性（Blackness）的征服与他者化之上；对2020–2025年ML、NLP与AI伦理领域十个顶级会议录用的69,290篇主会论文，开展两阶段、大语言模型辅助的内容分析。

Result: 安全研究占录用论文比例由2020年0.5%增至2025年6.4%，超过伦理研究；94%的安全论文依赖自动化指标，仅11%提及直接影响社会的危害（伦理论文为78%）；2,182篇安全论文中8篇提及种族，无一以之为核心，也无一提及黑人/非洲侨民群体；在识别出的254篇种族聚焦论文中，仅3篇以安全话语描述种族危害，55%集中于仅占语料2.5%的三家伦理会议。

Conclusion: AI安全领域系统性回避反黑人主义，其"安全"议程与社会危害脱节且高度集中于少数伦理场域。作者主张，公正的技术未来必须以直接直面反黑人主义为前提，并以跨学科视角重审AI安全。

Abstract: "AI safety" has grown from a niche contingent of actors into the dominant framing for artificial intelligence (AI) assessment and evaluation, overshadowing AI ethics. Through political and monetary influence, AI safety has shifted political focus within the US and abroad. The focus has shifted away from harms that currently affect the public toward threats framed as "existential risks". We argue that safety cannot be achieved while anti-Blackness is left unaddressed. We ground this argument in Sylvia Wynter's critique of the "overrepresentation" of Man: the modern, Western conception of the Human that is predicated on the subjugation and othering of Blackness. To analyze the state of addressing anti-Blackness in the age of AI safety, we conduct a two-stage, language model-assisted content analysis of 69,290 accepted main-track papers from ten leading machine learning (ML), natural language processing (NLP), and AI ethics conferences (2020--2025). Safety research grew from 0.5% of accepted papers in 2020 to 6.4% in 2025, overtaking ethics. Yet 94% of safety papers rely on automated metrics, and only 11% of safety papers mention harms that affect society directly, compared with 78% of ethics papers. Of the 2,182 safety papers, 8 mention race; none make it central, and none name Black or African-diaspora communities. Across all 254 identified as race-focused papers, only 3 describe racial harm in safety terms, and 55% appear at three ethics venues that publish 2.5% of the corpus. We offer this interdisciplinary examination of AI safety to argue that a just technological future depends on confronting anti-Blackness directly.

</details>


### [232] [AI-Driven Transformation of Digital Platform Work: A Task-Based Model for Measuring Labour Disruption and Vulnerability, with Early Evidence from Online Freelancing in Kenya](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25585)
*Farhan Latif*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于任务的分析框架，将"AI驱动的颠覆"（工作方式改变的程度）与"生计脆弱性"（伴随该改变的生计风险）区分开，并应用于平台工作来评估自动化、替代与增强的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于AI对劳动影响的评估多依赖传统职业分类或宏观市场指标，掩盖了同一职业内部任务随工作安排不同而变化的差异；在线自由职业者远程工作、大量时间从事无酬任务，这种局限尤为突出。

Method: 构建基于任务的框架，区分AI驱动的颠覆与生计脆弱性；将其应用于写作、虚拟助理、转录、网络与软件开发四类平台工作，评估自动化、替代和增强潜力；并将模型估计与肯尼亚内罗毕52名在线自由职业者的问卷调查及20次后续访谈进行对比。

Result: 模型估计四类工作的颠覆程度均较高，而生计脆弱性得分较低且因职业而异；无酬任务上的潜在效率提升部分抵消了有酬工作相关的潜在工资影响。调查显示工人报告任务执行方式改变、竞争加剧，并通过技能调整与策略性使用AI影响其对失业的担忧。

Conclusion: 基于任务的建模可为评估平台工作中的AI驱动变化提供基线；工人的实际体验与模型预测存在差异，有助于解释这些变化如何可能威胁其生计。

Abstract: Assessments of AI's impact on labour often rely on conventional occupational categories or broad market indicators. This dominant trend, however, obscures how tasks within the same occupation vary in practice across work arrangements. This limitation is particularly important in online freelancing, where workers are typically remote and devote substantial time performing unpaid tasks. This article introduces a task-based framework that distinguishes AI-driven disruption, defined as the extent of change in how work is performed, from livelihood vulnerability associated with that change. The framework is subsequently applied to assess the potential for automation, substitution, and enhancement across four categories of platform work: writing, virtual assistance, transcription, and web and software development. The model estimates high disruption across all four categories, while livelihood vulnerability scores are lower and vary by occupation. Potential efficiency gains on unpaid tasks partly offset potential wage impact associated with paid work in the model. The article compares these estimates with evidence from a survey of 52 online freelancers and 20 follow-up interviews in Nairobi, Kenya. Workers report changes in how they perform tasks and intensifying competition, while their accounts show how adapting their skills and using AI strategically shape their concerns about job loss. The findings show how task-based modelling can provide a baseline for assessing AI-driven changes in platform work and how workers' experiences differ from projections and help explain how these changes may threaten their livelihoods.

</details>


### [233] [KeyMem: Temporal Occupancy Control for Robust Streaming VideoLLM Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25649)
*Wei Song,Yuxin Cao,Xiao Cheng,Christy Jie Liang,Xi Zheng,Leo Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 提出训练-free的KeyMem包装器，用固定时长时间桶的硬性配额限制每段视频在视觉token缓存中的占比，把时序分配作为鲁棒性主控手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式VideoLLM的缓存基于分数淘汰，使视频流可自行决定时序占用，导致短时高显著片段垄断缓存、挤掉后续所需证据，形成"时序占用失控"根本缺陷。

Method: KeyMem：对任意基分数做秩归一化；将视频流切分为固定时长的时间桶并限制每桶可保留的token数（硬上限）；可选地用密钥对存活token采样；并提供decide-once变体。

Result: Qwen2.5-VL-7B上20s烧录字幕占8s记忆的84%，准确率由79.4%降至58.3%；五种自然压力源、三个VideoLLM、两种预算下，时序配额将较小预算下基于注意力保留的最差准确率由42.2%提升至73.3-76.1%，并在三个模型中两个上超过InfiniPot-V 6.1-16.7%；自然压力下确定性配额与密钥配额相当；自适应对手下密钥比泄露密钥多保4.4-9.5%早段准确率，但不稳定优于最佳确定性配额。

Conclusion: 时序分配是首要鲁棒性控制，密钥仅用于对抗可预测性。

Abstract: Streaming VideoLLMs retain a bounded cache of visual tokens. Yet score-based eviction lets the stream determine its own temporal occupancy, so a short salient segment can monopolize the cache and displace evidence needed later. We identify this uncontrolled temporal occupancy as a fundamental limitation and introduce KeyMem, a training-free wrapper that rank-normalizes any base score, divides the video stream into fixed-duration temporal buckets and limits how many visual tokens each bucket can retain, and optionally samples survivors with a secret key. This hard cap bounds a segment's memory share from its first chunk. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B, 20 s of burned-in captions occupies 84% of an 8 s memory and lowers accuracy over all clips from 79.4% to 58.3%. Across five natural stressors, three VideoLLMs and two budgets, temporal quotas raise the worst accuracy of attention-based retention at the smaller budget from 42.2% to 73.3-76.1% and exceed that of InfiniPot-V by 6.1-16.7% on two of the three models. Deterministic and keyed quotas perform comparably under natural stress. Under an adaptive adversary, a secret key preserves 4.4-9.5% more early-clip accuracy than a leaked key, but does not consistently beat the best deterministic quota. A decide-once variant keeps old evidence that fresh draws erode. Temporal allocation is the primary robustness control, with secrecy reserved for adversarial predictability.

</details>


### [234] [Neighbourhood Walkability and Cardiometabolic Morbidity in Greater London: A Fine-Grained Spatial Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26573)
*Benjamin T. Pike,Stephen Law,Sanja Šćepanović,Linus W. Dietz*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 在大伦敦4,994个普查单元上，步行适宜性与八种心血管代谢疾病患病率均呈负相关，但该关联在很大程度上由城市宏观空间结构（中心—边缘梯度、空间误差结构）介导，局部邻里效应的证据较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究普遍报告步行适宜性更高的邻里疾病患病率更低，但步行适宜性横跨多个空间尺度，全市尺度的关联可能把局部邻里形态与更广域的大都市结构及共变环境暴露混为一谈，因此需要显式区分全局与局部空间结构。

Method: 构建开放的五要素步行适宜性指数，覆盖4,994个大伦敦普查单元；以初级保健登记系统记录八种心血管代谢疾病患病率；控制人口构成与剥夺程度后做回归；进一步引入自治市（borough）内变异、中心—边缘径向模型（解释74.2%变异）调整以及空间误差模型；并与保留相同空间结构的替代步行适宜性曲面作对比，以及控制氮氧化物后的稳健性检验。

Result: 步行适宜性每提高一个标准差对应高血压患病率低1.43个百分点；八种结局均呈显著负相关。系数在自治市内下降2–43%，中心—边缘调整后下降11–75%；空间误差调整后肥胖无明确关联，其余七种系数相对可比的对数尺度模型下降83–99%。但观察关联仍比保留该空间结构的替代曲面更极端（主分析中八种结局均如此，控制氮氧化物后五种仍如此）。

Conclusion: 不应将全市尺度的步行适宜性系数解读为独立于大都市结构的邻里效应；邻里形态与健康研究须显式分离全局与局部空间结构，以避免尺度混杂导致的效应高估。

Abstract: Cardiometabolic diseases are leading causes of ill health, and neighbourhoods that support everyday walking may help reduce them. Studies often report lower disease prevalence in more walkable neighbourhoods. However, because walkability operates across multiple spatial scales, city-wide associations may conflate local neighbourhood form with broader metropolitan structure and co-varying environmental exposures. Here we show that while much of the walkability--morbidity association in Greater London operates through the city's macro-scale spatial structure, local alignment persists beyond it. Using an open five-component walkability index for 4,994 London census units and recorded prevalence of eight cardiometabolic conditions from primary-care registers, we found that higher walkability was inversely associated with all eight outcomes after adjusting for demographic composition and deprivation; one standard deviation higher walkability corresponded to 1.43 percentage points lower hypertension prevalence. Walkability followed a centre--periphery gradient, with a radial model explaining 74.2\% of its variation. Coefficients were reduced by 2--43\% within boroughs and 11--75\% after centre--periphery adjustment. After spatial-error adjustment, no clear association remained for obesity; coefficients for the other seven were reduced by 83--99\% relative to comparable log-scale models. Nevertheless, observed associations were more extreme than those from surrogate walkability surfaces preserving this spatial structure, for all eight outcomes in the primary analyses and for five after adjustment for nitrogen oxides. These findings caution against interpreting city-wide walkability coefficients as neighbourhood effects independent of wider metropolitan structure, and highlight the need for studies of neighbourhood form and health to disentangle global and local spatial structure explicitly.

</details>


### [235] [A Success Model at the End of Its Life Cycle: 30 Years of Regionalised Regional Rail in Germany](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26583)
*Lukas Iffländer*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 德国区域客运铁路"区域化"改革使客运周转量翻倍、运营成本下降，组织模式已进入生命周期末期，需启动第二轮改革。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1996年德国将区域客运铁路责任从联邦下放至各州；三十年实践虽总体成功，但招标竞争不足、机构重叠、车辆采购风险转嫁乘客、欧盟法院否定轨道使用费上限导致资金与订购运量不匹配，亟需评估并再改革。

Method: 基于三十年实践的政策回顾与制度分析：梳理区域化的动因与成效，识别三项结构性设计缺陷，并提出第二轮改革方案。

Result: 招标平均仅1.3家投标；26个采购主体与约60个交通联盟结构重叠；机车车辆采购反复将风险转嫁给乘客；2026年欧盟法院裁决后，资金不再覆盖订购服务量。

Conclusion: 建议整合采购主体、统一车辆标准、在"修复的市场模式"与"一体化公营运营"之间作出抉择，并使资金与基础设施实际成本发展挂钩（指数化）。

Abstract: On 1 January 1996, Germany transferred responsibility for regional passenger rail services from the federal government to the states. Three decades later, passenger-kilometres have doubled and competitive tendering has lowered operating costs substantially, making regionalisation the most successful element of the German rail reform. Its organisational model, however, has reached the end of its life cycle: tenders now attract on average only 1.3 bids, 26 contracting authorities and some 60 transport associations maintain overlapping structures, rolling-stock procurement has repeatedly shifted risk onto passengers, and since the Court of Justice of the European Union struck down the German track-access charge cap in 2026, funding no longer covers the ordered service volume. This article reviews the motives and results of regionalisation, identifies three structural design flaws, and argues for a second reform: consolidating authorities, standardising rolling stock, deciding between a repaired market model and integrated public operation, and indexing funding to real infrastructure cost development. The submission contains an English translation followed by the German original.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [LSP-RAG: Latent Scoring Perspectives for Knowledge Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25229)
*Haochen Liu,Guanghui Min,Song Wang,Yada Zhu,Chen Chen,Jundong Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出LSP-RAG框架，通过多视角提示打分增强轻量级模型在知识图谱上的多跳检索，实现低成本高性能的KG-RAG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM事实准确性不足且训练语料不完整，KG-RAG可缓解该问题；但现有方法要么依赖大模型（如ChatGPT）引导检索导致计算成本高，要么使用轻量模型时多跳场景检索质量受限。

Method: 提出LSP-RAG：以多视角提示打分实现逐跳（hop-wise）KG检索。每个潜在打分视角由两类软提示共同引导——视角专属软提示诱导出针对候选三元组的特定提示条件打分函数；上下文软提示编码查询与已探索三元组，随推理跳数演化以保持多跳一致性与相关性。各潜在视角无需人工定义语义角色，而是自适应聚合以捕捉异构相关性信号。

Result: 在多个基于KG的问答基准上优于现有检索系统，包括依赖更大规模语言模型的方法。

Conclusion: LSP-RAG使轻量级语言模型也能实现准确稳健的多跳检索，在有限计算预算下具备有效性与实用性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in open-domain question answering, but often struggle with factual accuracy and multi-hop reasoning due to the incompleteness of the training corpora. A promising solution is Knowledge Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (KG-RAG), which supplements LLMs with structured knowledge retrieved from external knowledge graphs (KGs). However, existing KG-RAG methods either rely on large-scale language models (e.g., ChatGPT) to guide the retrieval process, which leads to high computational costs, or suffer from limited retrieval quality when using lightweight language models, particularly under multi-hop scenarios. We propose LSP-RAG (Latent Scoring Perspectives for Retrieval-Augmented Generation over Knowledge Graphs), a novel KG-RAG framework that enhances hop-wise KG knowledge retrieval through multi-perspective prompted scoring. Each latent scoring perspective is guided by a combination of two types of soft prompts: a perspective-specific soft prompt induces a distinct prompt-conditioned scoring function over candidate triplets, while a contextual soft prompt evolves with each reasoning hop by encoding the query and previously explored KG triplets, enabling the model to preserve consistency and relevance across multi-hop retrieval. Rather than assuming manually defined semantic roles, these latent perspectives are adaptively aggregated to capture heterogeneous relevance signals during retrieval. This design allows LSP-RAG to perform accurate and robust retrieval using lightweight language models. LSP-RAG achieves superior performance across multiple KG-based Question Answering benchmarks compared to existing retrieval systems, including those that rely on much larger language models, demonstrating its effectiveness under limited computational budgets.

</details>


### [237] [Retrieving Patristic References and Modelling Topics in Bullinger's Reformation Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25247)
*Lena Pickartz,Philipp Schaer,Phillip B. Ströbel*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究布林格书信中教父引文的计算检测与细粒度主题建模：比较BM25、稠密双编码器与混合检索，并扩展FASTopic加入种子引导损失与编辑摘要嵌入。结果显示BM25在显式引文上最优（R@20 94%），混合融合将隐式引文R@20提升至22%（接近交叉编码器重排的26%但无推理开销）；主题模型连贯性增益微弱且主题权重接近均匀分布，难以用于检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海因里希·布林格（1504–1575）的书信大量援引教父著作作为权威来源，是宗教改革政治与神学辩论的重要史料，但此类引文的计算检测尚属空白；同时现有拉丁语互文研究仅在各自目标域微调稠密检索器，其跨域迁移能力未知，且缺乏对该语料的细粒度主题刻画手段。

Method: 1）检索：在显式与隐式引文上比较词法（BM25）、稠密（双编码器）与混合方法，并测试在圣经复用任务上微调的拉丁编码器向布林格书信的迁移性；分析BM25与稠密检索所命中隐式引文的集合差异并做排序融合，与交叉编码器重排对比。2）主题建模：在FASTopic中引入种子引导损失函数与编辑摘要嵌入；辅以质性分析与时间序列分析，并试点LLM概念归纳（LLooM）作为可解释替代方案。

Result: 在双编码器中，圣经复用微调的拉丁编码器表现最佳（显式R@20 82%，隐式16%）；BM25在显式引文上优于稠密方法（R@20 94%）。BM25与稠密检索恢复的隐式引文集合互不重叠，融合排序使隐式R@20升至22%，接近同候选集交叉编码器重排的26%，但避免了其推理成本。主题建模方面，相对基线的可测连贯性提升极小，部分主题在时间上可对应有记载的历史事件，但主导主题权重几乎不超过均匀分布，导致主题无法用于辅助检索；LLooM试点指向更具人类可解释性的方向。
